- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Plugin pages are set in type, few boxes: a bar of facts under the head (type, how hard it is to start, how much the agent does, who made it), then sections ruled apart: what it does (when to use it, who it is for, its highlights in two columns), what to say to your agent (one list, what the agent then does under each request, a click puts it in the chat), how to use it and its keys, and a quiet line on turning it off. The pages have no picture band for now.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Doubao: speech recognition with Doubao ASR 2.0 where the run can give the recording a web address (OpenFilm's service), the flash recognition still with your own key, and sound effects and music with Seed-Audio 1.0.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Replace the placeholder Doubao voice with the full official TTS 2.0 catalog, including multilingual voices. Use verified public provider recordings for cached, key-free previews, label missing previews clearly, and display the provider as Doubao.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Keep separate source audio rows in editing-app drafts, including rows whose clips do not overlap. Fail with the audio source name when a sound file is missing, unreadable, or has no audio stream, instead of silently omitting its clips from an otherwise successful editing-project export.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Dragging a keyframe moves it on the first try, even when it was not already selected.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Keep font-size input stable while custom text refits during preview. Allow horizontal group moves with existing overlaps, and move multiple clips up or down with Alt+ArrowUp/ArrowDown as one undoable overwrite, preserving the gaps in covered media. Probe footage referenced outside the root assets folder so its waveform and volume controls appear. Drag I/O-marked ranges from the source viewer onto the timeline with their original source in/out points.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Studio says editor where it said workbench: the menu at the top right is Editors, narrower; the card that says how an editor is used has no footer, its × closes it. A plugin's page sums it up by its type (an editor with its own interface, or a skill the agent uses in the chat), how hard it is to start and how much the agent does; every plugin is free, so cost is no longer shown, and an editor's own words say what turning it off does.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Recover stalled hardware video encoders by retrying in software, keep cancellation active during the final encoder flush, and report encoder changes while draft exports run. Handle background update download failures and disable QUIC to avoid Chromium transport errors in the desktop main process. Keep caption rows out of main-track magnet rules and keep captions and media aligned when a selection spanning tracks is moved.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Automatically open the export progress popover when an export is queued so its status is immediately visible.

- [#484](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/484) [`d132fe8`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/d132fe845bcea037ee7d1e2a477557cef3935692) Thanks [@hobostay](https://github.com/hobostay)! - Film: a numeric character reference outside Unicode (`&#x110000;`, `&#0;`, a surrogate such as `&#xD800;`) reads as U+FFFD, as a browser reads it, instead of throwing `RangeError: Invalid code point`. One such reference anywhere in a `film.html` made the whole film unopenable: Studio answered 500, and `openfilm look` and `openfilm render` stopped with the stack trace.

- [#483](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/483) [`2a15854`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/2a15854e2add6f74da8dc682d3a698104a554dc7) Thanks [@rick-mingyu-liu](https://github.com/rick-mingyu-liu)! - Google: a picture is made from the reference pictures given with `--ref` or wired in on the canvas, up to 14 (one for Flash-Lite Image), not from the prompt alone.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Keep an agent's info button beside its connection status when the row has no account description, avoiding a separate empty line.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Keep recorded audio attached to its video in native draft exports, including clips with rendered effects, retiming and fades. Separate audio clips and explicitly requested stems retain their existing behavior.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Star installed and discoverable skills, find them again under Favorites, and unstar them from either list.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Keep the chosen video aspect ratio for new video nodes while the canvas stays open.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Show a Back to chat pill above the composer when the library shares the chat panel. Move Open resource center to the bottom of the media sidebar as a plain text action.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Studio: every library tab's side bar has the same order: the media's Import, Favorites, Mine, Official and Community; the text's New text, Favorites, Mine (saved looks), Official and Community; every other tab's Favorites, Official and Community; the voice-over's Favorites, Mine and Services. A box shows all it holds and opens to its lines; Official is shown when OpenFilm has some of that kind, Community always, with Get from Community when nothing is there yet. Favorites gathers files starred in Mine with things starred in Official and the Community, each card saying where it is from. The Community lists only what people publish there, OpenFilm's own things marked OpenFilm ✓ like any publisher's; OpenFilm's built-in library is Official in the library and no longer in the Community. A media pack got from the Community stays under Community: its files are saved to a folder of Mine from their menu, the pack's or by dropping them on the folder. Examples listed in the Community may sit at the top of their own repository.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Separate interactive video effects from full-quality rendering. Add adaptive local playback drafts, debounced precise
  stills, latest-frame scheduling, independent analysis caches, identical-input inference reuse, and reusable GPU textures.
  Keep the selected cutout/beauty settings and export resolution independent of preview strategy.

  Expose automatic, smooth and quality-priority previews and detailed-preview rendering with progress, pause, cancellation
  and cache clearing. Yield background rendering to playback and editing, persist completed previews, and invalidate
  changed caption pictures while preserving renders when edits are outside their interval.

  Constrain detected face interiors as foreground before edge refinement so blown highlights in skin do not become
  transparent holes, and retain the face constraint and connected-person cleanup in interactive refined-person drafts.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Fix local model downloads and inference failing to start in the desktop app. Failed downloads now show a reason on the model page and can be retried.

- [#481](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/481) [`55215e9`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/55215e99b58d1e535a8c4dcdb78cd76ff96747a8) Thanks [@amine-boutouil](https://github.com/amine-boutouil)! - Studio: in the desktop app, Install in Settings → Services → Local services downloads the model again. Studio runs there on the app's own runtime, and the download (and each local transcript or background removal) was started as the app instead of as Node, so it ended at once and nothing happened. It now starts as Node; the `openfilm` command on Node is unchanged.

- [`ab67089`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/ab67089f3c6aebbdb917ce479a84a45bab9dd216) Thanks [@WEBGRS](https://github.com/WEBGRS)! - Local transcripts and background removal start on Windows: there the runtime is told its folder as a file: address and runs on one thread. Other systems are unchanged.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Disconnect works for a local service you added that takes no key (one that runs on your computer, such as a local voice server). Before, Settings → Services kept showing it connected and still sent voice-over to it. Now it stays off until you connect it again, and Connect does not ask for a key. The card of a service you added says so, instead of showing a raw text key.

- [`4c2264c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4c2264c1cb0c70ba09d2bab37b84f9cd0d521746) Thanks [@WEBGRS](https://github.com/WEBGRS)! - Studio: a project that uses a recording of hours opens and plays. A sound longer than thirty minutes is played as it streams instead of being decoded whole, which held some 5 GB for a 3-hour recording and crashed the editor's page, and a sound request the browser gives up while its copy is being made no longer stops Studio.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A sound longer than about an hour (a two-hour recording) has its waveform again: its samples are made into peaks as ffmpeg reads them, never held whole in memory.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - `openfilm look <t>` writes one frame as a JPEG at most 1568 px on its long edge, so an agent can keep several in a conversation without running into a model service's request limit. A frame with transparency stays a PNG, at most as large. Contact sheets are unchanged.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - `openfilm look` takes several times and ranges at once (`look 8.4-8.8 26.5 35.9-36.3`) and draws them in one sheet, opening the film once, so an agent checks every cut of a film, or picks through long footage, in one look instead of one look per frame. Before, only the last time or range given was drawn. A video of 30 s or more with sound and no transcript beside it says the one command that gives its words (`openfilm get asr --src …`), so what is said is read there instead of from frame after frame. The film and color skills no longer tell an agent to look at every shot or clip after each change: it looks as closely as the work needs, one moment or clip, several in one look, or footage that speaks by its words first.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Reduce editing draft size with exact PNG packet deduplication, cropped transparent layers, and verified lossless RGB runs on the tested macOS Jianying 11.8.0 decoder. Preserve the original frame clock, alpha, source audio and editable timeline placement. Reuse completed clip media through a content-checked cache without keeping a hidden media duplicate, and expose per-clip render and postprocessing timings.

  Reuse original full-frame footage when its static CSS is proved neutral. Encode transparent graphics with lossless QuickTime Animation on the tested decoder, keep PNG when it is smaller, and verify the full RGBA stream and timestamps before replacing media. Remove only exact RLE no-op packets. Reuse static caption captures between cue boundaries, retaining every output frame and falling back for motion, highlighting, external styles and scripts.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - The editing manual now says that Studio adds a caption track by itself when the first words timed by word come in, not only when the person asks.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A made file that cannot be fetched from its address is tried again at the other addresses OpenFilm gives for it, so a path to one that fails no longer stops its delivery.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Make the empty import card and media pane reliably accept multiple dropped files, including drag stores exposing
  files through data items. Show the import target during dragover and prevent the project-wide drop handler from
  also inserting or importing the files. Preserve folder-target drops and internal tile moves.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A paid run is not lost or paid twice when things go wrong. A job fal, OpenRouter or Volcengine takes on your own key is kept as it is taken, and waited for again when the same run starts after a restart. A project that opens with paid runs left uncollected says so and collects them, never starting one that was not taken. A transfer that stops moving is cut and resumed where it stopped, however long a moving one takes, and a run with OpenFilm is still found after signing out and in again.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Stream OpenFilm reference uploads and generated-file downloads, resume interrupted downloads with integrity checks, and keep pending request identities across client restarts so recovery retrieves the original paid task. Confirm delivery only after local files are saved; `--fresh` explicitly requests a new take. Forward native vendor result URLs and stream binary-only music to the service's temporary relay. Report expired results instead of treating a receipt-only replay as delivery.

  Keep an unfinished canvas generation's output name across retries and app reloads, so clicking Make again can recover its original OpenFilm task. Stop a batch after recovering its pending result instead of silently creating additional takes.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A paid run the OpenFilm service refused (no balance, a limit, the model failing) no longer comes back the same each time: the same settings make a new run once topped up or retried; a run with no answer from the service is still kept to recover. Studio waits for an OpenFilm video as long as the service can take to make it, short requests that hear nothing are asked again sooner, and a voice spoken from the voice library is recovered after an interruption instead of made and paid again.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Dropping files into Mine in the desktop app no longer leaves a card at 0% behind each one.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A picture's anchor point: `--fx-ax` and `--fx-ay` (stage px from its box's middle) are where it turns and scales
  about, its box's turn too, keyed as its effects are (SPEC §1).

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Animate: cutting or animating is two icons first on the timeline's toolbar. A page's element picked on the timeline
  is in hand on the picture, its frame and handles; the layer tree has its guides, a twirl beside each name that holds
  others, and a page's words split a character an element are one layer. Layers' bars take their label's color and
  mark their keyframes, and a clip's bar marks its keyframes when cutting too. The edit's Position section opens the
  clip in Animate, its folder and properties twirled open; with nothing in hand, Animate shows how a move is made.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Add caption tracks directly from the captions panel or a caption track's menu. New tracks start with an editable
  caption above the existing captions, and adding a caption uses the selected caption track. Empty caption tracks stay
  on the timeline after their last caption is moved or deleted, so captions can be dragged back onto them.
  Cross-track drags keep the caption's region position and selection, including when both tracks use the same region name.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Editors', connectors' and skills' pages are laid out alike: how hard it is and how much the agent does, then What it does, How to use it (numbered steps, where to open it first), What you can say (lines put in the chat by a click), and one quiet line on what turning it off does. The For developers fold is gone, and so is the Type measure (it is said under the name). Mine turns each thing on or off with a switch instead of a gear menu; removing it is on its page. Screening's speed and full-screen buttons are drawn as its play and volume buttons are. A new conversation opens on the question, a line of what to do and one list of the library and the Community.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - The film's sound has one engine: the limiter that keeps every film under -2 dBFS (and the loudness gain before it) is now one JavaScript function that the preview runs in an AudioWorklet and the export runs in Node, so what Studio plays of a mix is what the export delivers of it, sample for sample. Played from the middle, the preview first feeds it the second before, as the export has it there. The limiter catches the peaks between samples too, leaves everything under the ceiling exactly as mixed (a quiet film is exported bit for bit as its mix; before, it went through a resampler), and holds its gain through low notes, so loud bass distorts far less. Studio's preview now plays at 48 kHz, the export's rate.

- [#466](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/466) [`e983acc`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/e983acce3a3ce2eb22b3959248d3aaae74370cf8) Thanks [@WEBGRS](https://github.com/WEBGRS)! - Studio: on Windows an open project can be renamed again after its agent has finished a turn. The chat's agent stays running for a while between turns, working in the project's folder, and Windows will not rename a folder a process works in, so every rename failed with "Rename failed. Please retry." until another project was opened. The agent between turns now stops before the rename (its next turn starts in the renamed folder), a folder that is only held for a moment is tried again, and one that something else still holds is refused with a message that says so and what to close.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Send media files with spaces in their names when using OpenFilm's service, including video first and last frames, reference images and transcription recordings. Report an unreadable input locally before submitting the run.

  Generated media names also accept spaces, punctuation, emoji and all languages, including combining characters. Reject names that cannot be saved across supported file systems before starting a paid request.

  Keep these names when making another take, fit the numbered name within file system limits, and avoid collisions between equivalent Unicode spellings.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A page with more than 200 layers takes changes to all of them: moving, hiding or deleting many layers at once no longer fails with "overrides has to be a list (at most 200)".

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Studio: pasted layers are selected on a slow computer too, where the page they were pasted into takes a while to open.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Use Studio's themed menus and color pickers on Windows and macOS for voice filters, voice cloning, library actions, motion controls, chroma keys and lighting. Keep nested menus above their dialogs, inside the screen, and separate their keyboard controls from film shortcuts.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Every editor's player has the same play, speed and full-screen buttons (the player's icons, sized to its bar); one whose state is not the default (a menu open, a speed other than 1×, a loop, full screen) sits on a pressed background. In the Community, Connect, Get, Installed, Off and Connected each have a look of their own. A local model being downloaded can be cancelled, and an installed one uninstalled, from its page and its line. Doubao's two podcast pairs can be heard: each has a short dialogue of ours, made with its voices (Doubao publishes no sample of them). A new conversation opens on the question in the middle, with chips for the library and the Community.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - OpenFilm's own plugins bring their workbench's build entry and their own words: the workbenches Studio's page and the desktop app's window build are read from each plugin's `browser` module (its source in `studio/ui/src/plugins/<id>/`), and a plugin's `i18n/<language>.json` words are looked up as `plugin.<id>.…`, in English where a language leaves one out. An official plugin's workbench other than the edit and the canvas now shows its element in the panes' place.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Use the backquote/tilde key for preview fullscreen and Shift-backquote for linkage, migrate legacy default bindings,
  and show active shortcut bindings in toolbar tooltips, including custom schemes and fullscreen controls.

  Add optional local ViTMatte person edge refinement and BiSeNet semantic face regions, clarify low-precision cutout,
  and add texture retention, blemish/highlight reduction, rotation-aligned face shaping and glasses protection.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Make project management work like a file browser, with a folder sidebar, navigation history, compact grid and detail views, direct selection, keyboard shortcuts, drag and drop, and batch moves and deletion. Remove the separate selection mode.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - The projects window drops its title row: search, Examples and where projects are kept now sit in its sidebar, and the storage settings (the projects folder, what projects take, clearing the cache) open from there. Settings → General starts with your account: its picture, name and email once signed in, with a way to manage it on the website; not signed in is a local account where everything works.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Allow generation inputs to read media the user linked into a project, including file and folder references on Windows shares. Project-supplied links still require local permission, and linked originals remain read-only.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A file of Mine can be pointed at for your agent: its menu has "Reference in chat", which
  brings the file into the project first, so the agent is given a file it can use as it is. A file a service made tells
  the agent the prompt, service and model it was made with, so "one more like this, at night" works.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Show Studio's current automatic render concurrency and allow an explicit browser limit for video, GIF and editing-project exports. Share the selected limit across all pages in an editing project, retain the conservative Auto recommendation, and report the selected browser limit and per-page workers alongside render timings. Preserve the original output dimensions, frame clock and lossless media paths.

  Pipeline editing-project drawing with a CPU-sized postprocessing pool capped at four tasks. Keep waiting pages inside the browser budget, propagate cancellation through the bounded queue, and report postprocessing wait time separately.

- [`76df36d`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/76df36dee8a9c818f2bcfdc61ecfec8e2c09ef84) Thanks [@e3chovivi](https://github.com/e3chovivi)! - Render: a `--scale` that lands between two pixels writes an even picture. `--scale .667` made the browser's surface 1281 wide for a 1920 film: the MP4 was written, and re-encoding it failed with `width not divisible by 2` in every strict H.264 encoder, so the file could not go on to an editor or a second pass. The odd last column or row is cropped on the way in, so every other pixel stays as drawn; a scale that already lands even is untouched, and the summary now names the size the file actually has.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - The library's shapes repeat with a start and an end opacity, as an animation editor's repeater does: each copy as
  far from the first's opacity to the last's as it is among the copies, both keyable under the layer's Contents.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Studio: the library drawer's side bar is namespaces, boxes as compact as an editor's: a box picks, or opens to the lines it holds, and stays as it was left. The media's are Import (this project) and Mine (the person's folders, those of the library folder, made there or linked from another disk or a NAS; one alone is Mine itself, more open under it, in the order the person drags). The media's "+ Add" makes or links a folder, gets media from the Community, says how to make media and contributes them (each folder uploads in its own pane). Every other category starts with Favorites, the things starred in it (a star on each card and row), then Official (OpenFilm's groups) and Community (packs added), the text's word art and templates; "+ Add" at its foot opens upward with getting from the Community, how to make things, contributing them and putting back a pack taken out. The voice-over's are Mine, Favorites and Services (all, then each service); Mine has tabs on the right: the person's voices, Record and Clone, a voice cloned in place. Files dropped on a folder go in it: from this computer, from another folder, from the project, or a thing of a pack copied there. The "+" at the side bar's foot uploads files, makes or links a folder, gets more from the Community, says how to make things and contributes them; a pack is taken out of the library from its menu and put back from "+". The projects window has a side bar: the OpenFilm account at its top (signed in, its picture and name; else the local account and Sign in), then Projects and Examples, and Start with Edit or Canvas (a new project opened on that workbench); at its foot the Community's news and this version, with what is new in it and the update when one is ready. Projects open on New project, Open folder and Get from GitHub side by side, then all projects under a bar that stays as they scroll, its tools (search, new folder, sort, view, more, the Recycle Bin) at its end. Settings › General has Storage as one row, what OpenFilm takes and what is free, opening its own page. In OpenFilm's own Recycle Bin, what is deleted in OpenFilm (files, folders, projects, skills, voices, subjects) can be restored, deleted permanently or emptied; something on another disk still goes to that disk's system trash. Save to puts clips and files into one of the person's folders. The first time, what lay loose at the library's top moves into the asset library. In the chat, "+" opens a compact menu lined up with it, with files or photos (⌘U), a folder, skills and references; `/` names a skill and `@` points at things, and the Community button is back beside the library's. An empty conversation asks what to make, with a line under it for each of the two buttons at the message box's right, with their marks: the Community (plugins, skills and material to add) and the library (media to import, templates, music and effects), each opening it.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Studio: the resource center lists only places connected from outside OpenFilm, with Projects and the asset library fixed first in its sidebar, and files used from them under recent; a new folder is made in the library itself. A view of no one folder offers no import, and adding to a collection is on a file's right-click menu. Settings → Storage shows what OpenFilm takes: projects and linked projects (media, versions, conversations, files), the library, skills, exports, cache and the app's own folders, measured once and refreshed after a clear. The add-to-project choices use Studio's menus, and the chat's way back sits on the drawer without shading it.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Retry media imports while Windows temporarily holds their staging files. Reusing an identical imported file now succeeds even if redundant staging cleanup stays locked, and cleanup failures preserve the original import error.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A plugin's page shows the editor at work (a picture drawn from its own interface), four things it does, three conversations with your agent (what you say, what it then does, a button that puts it in the chat), how it works step by step and its keys; plugins describe these in an optional `showcase` (`highlights`, `keys`) and `examples` may say what the agent does (`{ say, does }`). Layouts offer what each editor's work wants: the edit Default, Library (the library beside the work), Detail (its layers open) and Focus; the canvas Default, Library and Focus; Screening and Color Default and Focus. The layout menu no longer lists the panels under its layouts (Edit layout has them). The canvas's sound card is laid out as a recorder: thin waveform bars with a playhead, the time large, a round play button.

- [`a9a3d50`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/a9a3d50b6ca22e541521f17a8b342d02a40f090d) Thanks [@WEBGRS](https://github.com/WEBGRS)! - Clips and groups saved in the library keep the @keyframes they play, so they still move where they are put. `openfilm get` prints them too.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - In the app, the person's service keys are kept sealed in `~/.openfilm/providers.json` with a key only the system's keychain opens for the app (macOS Keychain, Windows DPAPI), so an agent, skill or plugin command reading the file gets nothing usable. Keys kept plain by an older OpenFilm or `npx openfilm` are sealed when the app starts; those builds still read and write the file, but do not see the sealed keys. Each sealed key opens only for the service it was saved for. Studio's own key stays in the app's memory: `run.json` names only the agents' key, which the `openfilm` command sends as it is, and `openfilm open` gives an agent an address with its own key. The app's own agent gets its models' keys over a private pipe, not its environment, so no command it runs sees them, and Claude Code is told not to read Studio's key files. `npx openfilm` on its own still keeps keys plain, readable by this user only.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - What agents read is shorter and says only what is true. `openfilm get` offers an agent the library's music, sound effects and stickers; transitions, effects, filters, texts and templates stay in Studio's library for you to pick. `openfilm --help` leaves out `open` and `update` for an agent in the desktop app, and no longer lists the canvas's commands as OpenFilm's own: the canvas skill says them. The media skill is half as long, `openfilm skill <name>` prints a skill without its front matter, the app's own agent is told which plugins are off as other agents are, and `openfilm edit` points to each command's `--help` rather than to a skill it does not have.

- [#485](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/485) [`f9e4ee9`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/f9e4ee91ea0578974f1796a4ab54fd65e646f6de) Thanks [@hobostay](https://github.com/hobostay)! - Studio: opened at `localhost` or `[::1]`, the editor goes to Studio's own `127.0.0.1` address. The page used to load and sign in on the alias, but every write was refused ("not from Studio") because a write's Origin had to be exactly `http://127.0.0.1:<port>`, and the film origin's CORS header named that origin too, so previews' media was blocked. The key the caller showed travels along; one it did not show is not added.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - When Studio adds a caption track of what is said to film.html by itself, or writes that track again, the agent's next `openfilm` command (or the chat's next message) says so in one line, so the change is not a surprise.

- [`cf86d10`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/cf86d10783453e033b4c6559e3e6564fe178b0a2) Thanks [@WEBGRS](https://github.com/WEBGRS)! - Studio: a talking head exported to another editing app stays one clip with its own sound. A video filling the frame (`object-fit: cover`, framed anywhere by `object-position`) is placed by the editor's own scale and position instead of being drawn into a new file with its sound split off beside it, and a style that only says what CSS does anyway (`object-fit: contain`, `opacity: 1`, as choosing Fit in the inspector writes) no longer counts as a look of its own. A fill its box cuts inside the frame, a stretch and any other look are drawn as before.

- [#471](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/471) [`b3cdd47`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/b3cdd47c890ed48bc7571746d8c91e0f1c58da5a) Thanks [@Novakru](https://github.com/Novakru)! - Let video waveforms grow with their tracks while keeping the audio band at least 15 px tall.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Let a selected clip's trim handles take priority at shared cuts. Keep magnetic head trims under the pointer while dragging, then close the gap and move following clips on release.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - The edit and the canvas are the ways of working on a project; animating is the edit's timeline shown another way,
  picked first on its toolbar (an icon and a menu, as the tool picker beside it): Edit for clips, cuts and transitions,
  Animate for layers, keyframes and easing.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Keep muted videos' audio bands and volume handles visible, with a mute indicator. Keep volume handles inside the clip at silence and full scale so they remain draggable. Offer Unmute in the audio inspector to restore the original 0 dB level.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Voice-over and captions are categories of the library again, beside the media, as before. Voice-over's side bar lists all voices, your own, the starred, each service's, and Record: recording from the microphone moved there from the timeline's toolbar. It starts at the playhead and puts the sound on a sound track as before, goes on while the library shows another category (voice-over's icon has a red dot meanwhile), and voice-over opens on it again. Captions' side bar is From speech, Subtitle file and By hand (a new caption track, or one caption at the playhead), in place of its source switch. In the library's side bar the project's line is called Import and "+" reads "+ Add". A folder or a pack is moved by holding it and moving it up or down: it follows the pointer while the others make room, Esc puts it back, and it is in its place the moment it is let go. Edit and Canvas have new icons (scissors, linked cards), and a plugin's changed icon shows at once instead of after the hour the page kept the old one.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Volcengine: Seedance 2.5 Flash makes 480p and 720p clips with sound of 4 to 30 s, from up to 10 pictures and 5 clips and recordings, and Seedream 5.5 makes pictures at 1K or 2K from the prompt or from up to 10 pictures, transparent when asked. Settings → Services now describes Volcengine.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Studio in a browser: the picture's top-left button opens and closes the library (Media, Voices, Captions, Templates and the rest), which has no other button there without the app's chat. The layout menu shows each panel of the arrangement as a switch (the edit's Layers, Inspector and Timeline), so Layers opens from there.

- [#496](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/496) [`bd1359c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bd1359c40dc479f05e6867cb50ee434a9526802c) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - On Windows, a linked folder that moved can be pointed at its new place again, by hand or when Studio finds it, and another community index can be a file on this computer named by its drive (C:\…).

## 0.3.2

### Patch Changes

- Refresh canvas models as soon as a media service connects or its model changes in Settings, including sign-in completed in the browser.
- Keep the assets drawer and workspace aligned with the chat divider while resizing. Show in Assets scrolls to and briefly highlights the canvas file without changing the editing selection.
- Attach text, images and video references to canvas writing nodes. Organize compatible timeline tracks with undo, and keep picture and sound solo selections independent.
- 7c6bf43: Show mixed audio waveforms on compound clips, including templates. Mark compound clips on the timeline and offer an Open compound clip action in the context menu.
- 7c6bf43: Duplicate a project from the Projects menu to open an independent copy of its current edit in the same project group.
- 03168c3: Studio: example films and download estimates over an hour show hours instead of a minute count past 60
- 7c6bf43: Keep export progress in the project title bar, with a task list that opens above any workspace layout. Give voice previews a circular orb that carries over the voice avatar's light spots and pulses with the audio.
- 7344f84: Studio: export sizes near 1 MB and 1 GB no longer read 1024 KB or 1024 MB
- 7c6bf43: Mark transparent HEVC exports as straight alpha so editors that honor the alpha mode blend soft glows and edges at the intended opacity.
- 7c6bf43: Keep editable subtitle font size, installed font, weight, color and outline when exporting a 剪映 or CapCut draft.
  Keep copied and rendered media linked when a 剪映 draft is renamed.
- 7c6bf43: Keep static rotation, opacity, and simple hard-edged rectangle masks editable in Jianying drafts. More complex clip effects keep their rendered appearance.
- 7c6bf43: Keep masks, crops and other rendered clip effects when exporting an editing project in automatic effects mode. Render the part used on the timeline and keep full-length footage for further editing, converting containers such as WebM when needed.
- 454426e: Studio: the library category list no longer shows a category twice when a saved order repeats it
- 7c6bf43: Show a circular loading indicator while library assets load and offer retry when a download fails, load sticker animations only on click, and use the viewer's transport for asset previews. Clicking the timeline restores the project viewer. Keep the add button visible while cached media opens, and add assets with the button, keyboard or drag. Hover on a visual card to preview it in place. Template previews share one cached video between the card and viewer. Moving away stops playback but leaves downloads running. Audio previews start on click. Voices show their download status in the same position as the use button, and sticker cards are larger. Voice previews animate the colors of each voice’s avatar in the viewer. Sounds and music show their own artwork. Clicking empty space dismisses the selected preview.
- 805c947: Studio: workspace preset thumbnails in a narrow window no longer draw the inspector outside the picture
- 7c6bf43: Link Windows media files and folders even when a network share refuses system links. The project's references keep the originals in place and work for preview and export. Use Alt to toggle whole clips and their layers together in the Layers panel, or Shift to select a range by clicking or dragging. OpenFilm's agent asks for sign-in when its credential is rejected and keeps technical service errors in its connection details.
- 27be758: Studio: editing-project exports keep the file name of footage written with Windows backslash paths
- 7c6bf43: Add TBC mischief 1 and Grok Imagine Video 1.5 Lite to the OpenRouter video models in Studio and the CLI.
- 7c6bf43: Open projects pick up changes to the timeline even if the system misses a file notification. The editor retries a failed refresh, keeps newer edits over older responses, and continues to refresh after a save and its recovery read both fail. Keep the editing timeline, film preview and canvas mounted at their own workspace sizes while switching between them.
- 7c6bf43: Refresh a paused preview when a page's images finish loading so image-based cards can appear without moving the playhead.
- d23613a: On Windows, an editing-software export whose browsers crash draws those pages again instead of failing with a permission error.
- 7c6bf43: Always keep effects and include visible timeline subtitles when exporting an editing project. Use the Settings palette in the export dialog.
- 8ef4514: Studio: the Spanish editing-export hint names the application in all three places, as the other four languages do
- 7c6bf43: Show the first frame of canvas videos when idle, share their sound setting, and restart playback on each hover. Show media import progress in place and add frame capture and draggable video headers with inline renaming. Keep the model picker compact and make the side buttons easier to see and use.
- c6bc8e9: Studio: a key from another character set is refused with a forbidden, not a server error
- 7c6bf43: Load thumbnails for visible media cards before scanning the rest of the project. This keeps large projects from opening with an empty row of previews.
- 38b79d0: Studio: What's New is shown again when a beta.2 build is followed by beta.10

## 0.3.1

### Patch Changes

- Editing-project exports for Premiere Pro, Final Cut Pro, Jianying and CapCut can use complete original footage, by reference or full copy, preserving source in/out handles, native frame rates, playback speeds and disabled clips. Rendered-effects export remains available; web pages still render when needed.

- The canvas module preloads after the first paint and warms in the background after opening a project. Its first display has a loading view, hidden warming does not save a board, and returning to the edit keeps the canvas hidden.

- Timeline ranges have a visible close button and clear with Escape even when clips are selected. Open menus keep their Escape priority.

- Layout → Edit layout opens a temporary editor for the current work mode. Move any panel into nested splits, swap panels, dock at workspace edges and resize live. Done saves the layout; Cancel restores it, with undo and redo while editing. The library retains its original UI without an extra header. Edit and canvas presets stay within their own mode, and canvas resizing preserves the current view center. Named layouts support import and export.

- Click a library asset to download its missing files and preview automatically, including stickers, community effects and templates. Download icons are passive status indicators. Compact loading bars indicate the wait without extra explanatory text. Previews can be cancelled or retried and ignore late events from previous previews. Template videos start automatically when ready and stop when closed. Background waveform downloads leave connections available for playback.

- Settings keeps Codex usage percentages on one line and lists Google Antigravity and DeepSeek Harness first among optional agents.

- Disable or enable selected timeline clips with Shift+E or the clip menu, with undo and redo. Their source trims, levels and neighbouring clips stay in place; disabled clips are neither drawn nor heard, and export to editing projects disabled.

- Jianying and CapCut drafts retain complete video and audio sources and native playback speeds. Processed footage retains the full source duration and editable trim handles, with an untouched source in the media library.

- Transparent exports resize and average motion-blur samples using alpha-weighted color, preventing bright fringes. Switching between transparent and opaque PNG frames no longer resets the sampling filters and drops output frames.

- [#375](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/375) [`46811b0`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/46811b0a2359666cfc03e3dd6f64e0f1e08db764) Thanks [@WEBGRS](https://github.com/WEBGRS)! - Back on the edit from the canvas, the canvas's cards no longer stay on top of the picture and the inspector.

- [#381](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/381) [`b108090`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/b1080902e6847f55323dbc092981c0cc685a4578) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Undoing a chat turn restores its canvas boards along with its files. A turn that only changes the canvas can be undone too. Open boards refresh after the restore and forget undo steps that could bring back references to files the turn's undo removed.

- [#376](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/376) [`c4e178a`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/c4e178ae5aaf2a9a61342b32f530e1bff006214f) Thanks [@WEBGRS](https://github.com/WEBGRS)! - Canvas characters: a picture is kept only from inside the project. On Windows a path written with backslashes could reach a picture outside it.

- [#372](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/372) [`873eb0e`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/873eb0e7257e354d38b5aac1930a4b868dc60ca3) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Studio and `openfilm get` can discover, preview and use reviewed community sounds, music, transitions and effects from the OpenFilm library.

- [#362](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/362) [`90fed06`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/90fed06b47e510c92ab31c1a8d5843e6901ab5c6) Thanks [@deepankar-sah](https://github.com/deepankar-sah)! - Desktop: support Google Antigravity as a coding agent via the Agent Client Protocol (ACP).

- [#385](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/385) [`111b513`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/111b51362e9d76c2f304bbdf1fe0f5cb88a42a73) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Local model installation is reported complete only after its provider settings have had a chance to refresh. A fast download could previously show a model as installed while its media kind was still unselected.

- [#384](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/384) [`574e6fe`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/574e6fee757e6e77fb49f050702f98ad23cefcde) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - The project title's hover card retries a poster that is still being drawn, using the same bounded delays as the Projects list. A first request that arrived too early used to leave its cover permanently blank.

- [#309](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/309) [`916e845`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/916e845614ff10c5360c9d4e057b1e3db138ec36) Thanks [@rick-mingyu-liu](https://github.com/rick-mingyu-liu)! - Render: a render that stops on its own (an error, a crash, the computer asleep or out of power, the app quit) is resumed by the next one of the same file with the same settings, which draws only what is left. The film is drawn in chunks of whole seconds, one to ten, that the browsers take in turn, and each chunk finished is kept with the files it was drawn from, those it asked for and did not find included: a render that finds one of them changed, or other settings, starts clean. Studio's export resumes the same way; one cancelled on purpose leaves nothing behind. Chunks no one resumes are removed after two weeks, and so are the parts a crashed render left before.

- [#348](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/348) [`af52c5c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/af52c5c096aab2584711e96fa505ba76477deb35) Thanks [@rick-mingyu-liu](https://github.com/rick-mingyu-liu)! - Restoring a version no longer stops half way when the trash will not take a file (on Windows, the Recycle Bin refusing one too big for it): the version is put back around it, the file stays where it is, and Studio names the files that stayed. Before, the files already written stayed written, the rest did not, and History still showed the version the person was on.

- [#377](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/377) [`4df6f94`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4df6f941cd6803f0b597994e30391be4531202ab) Thanks [@WEBGRS](https://github.com/WEBGRS)! - A transition clicked in the library plays once on the picture, and a click on it plays it again. It used to repeat until it was let go. Enter and "+" still put it in.

- [#361](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/361) [`9bdcbb5`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/9bdcbb54cf5bbe4e1975590301b537284ae8dc0f) Thanks [@WEBGRS](https://github.com/WEBGRS)! - Studio on Linux: deleting a file, a folder or a skill on another disk than the home folder (a project on a second drive) failed with "cross-device link not permitted". It is now copied into the trash with its record and then removed, so it can still be restored.

- [#374](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/374) [`8cea0e9`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/8cea0e949648ddc860b7fd0c92c65a14161c4869) Thanks [@WEBGRS](https://github.com/WEBGRS)! - Undo of a chat turn: the film goes back as the turn found it. Studio took the film put back for a write of the agent's and merged the turn's clips into it, so a clip came back whose page the undo had just moved to the trash.

- [#383](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/383) [`684d04d`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/684d04d2041d1442ec14d85d175e1413e7628d86) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A page is loaded again when Windows briefly has no network buffer for a module needed to initialize the film. Previously only a failed page navigation was retried, so a transient failure of captions or overrides could stop an export. Permanent errors are still reported without retrying.

- [#407](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/407) Thanks [@yunloong-Y](https://github.com/yunloong-Y)! - Studio: a clip with a fragment in its src still counts as using that file

- [#393](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/393) Thanks [@yunloong-Y](https://github.com/yunloong-Y)! - Studio: splitting a caption cue at its middle keeps an emoji whole, instead of writing half a character into each cue.

- [#402](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/402) Thanks [@yunloong-Y](https://github.com/yunloong-Y)! - Studio: a damaged character record no longer takes the whole characters panel down

- [#392](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/392) Thanks [@yunloong-Y](https://github.com/yunloong-Y)! - Studio: a cookie whose value is not percent-encoded is ignored, instead of stopping the request it came on.

- Discover shows its official offline snapshot on a new installation while checking the live skill index. Refreshes have a deadline, retain usable cached skills when one download fails, and can be retried in the gallery. Pinned file manifests avoid GitHub's anonymous API quota and detect incomplete downloads. Older responses no longer erase a newly refreshed skill list.

- [#386](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/386) Thanks [@yunloong-Y](https://github.com/yunloong-Y)! - doctor: a report takes keys and email addresses out of the Studio log lines it quotes, as it says it does

- [#396](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/396) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - HEVC master exports use finer grain-aware software encoding so subtle changing texture is not flattened into a still pattern. Master files can be larger and take longer to encode.

- [#388](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/388) Thanks [@yunloong-Y](https://github.com/yunloong-Y)! - Studio: a long file name is not cut in the middle of a character

- [#387](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/387) Thanks [@yunloong-Y](https://github.com/yunloong-Y)! - Studio: a byte range past a media file's end is answered, not left hanging

- [#395](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/395) Thanks [@yunloong-Y](https://github.com/yunloong-Y)! - Studio: a file replaced under the same name, size and date gets a new preview, instead of the old one being kept.

- [#405](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/405) Thanks [@yunloong-Y](https://github.com/yunloong-Y)! - Render: a resumed render does not reuse chunks drawn from a file that was replaced

- [#404](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/404) Thanks [@yunloong-Y](https://github.com/yunloong-Y)! - CLI: a SKILL.md with empty front matter keeps its body and name

- [#389](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/389) Thanks [@yunloong-Y](https://github.com/yunloong-Y)! - CLI: a skill named with capitals is found by its name alone

- [#403](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/403) Thanks [@yunloong-Y](https://github.com/yunloong-Y)! - CLI: a sound whose name contains a percent sign is found instead of stopping look and render

- [#394](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/394) Thanks [@yunloong-Y](https://github.com/yunloong-Y)! - Studio: a template's object-position is read as CSS reads it, so its framing control starts where the picture actually sits.

- [#406](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/406) Thanks [@yunloong-Y](https://github.com/yunloong-Y)! - Studio: an rgba() color in a template keeps its alpha when its scheme changes

- [#280](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/280) Thanks [@rick-mingyu-liu](https://github.com/rick-mingyu-liu)! - While OpenFilm is not in front, the system says when the chat's agent has finished a turn, stopped with an error, or waits for your answer (a question or a permission). Clicking the notification brings the window back on that project's tab; the taskbar button flashes on Windows and the Dock icon bounces on macOS. Settings › General › Notifications switches them off. An error is shown without your home folder, keys or email addresses.

## 0.3.0

### Minor Changes

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - The assets drawer has a library: its categories along the top (Media, Voices, Sounds, Music, Text, Stickers, Filters, Effects, Transitions, Templates) and each one's groups down the left, OpenFilm's own transitions, effects, filters, text, stickers and sounds free to use in any film. Point at a thing and it plays; click it to try it on the picture in the viewer; double-click it, press Enter or click its + to put it in at the playhead (a transition on the cut, a filter on the picked clips, else the clip under the playhead), then change it in the inspector. Drag a filter onto a clip, or a dissolve onto a cut. Star what you use often: it is in Favorites in every project.

  Voices lists the ready-made voices of ElevenLabs (Eleven v4), Inworld (TTS-2) and Cartesia (Sonic 3.6), as each service lists them, by service, language and gender. Click one to hear its sample; double-click it, press Enter or click its + to put it in the chat, and your agent makes the voice-over in it. A voice whose service is not connected yet says where to connect it.

  Media in the library is what came into the project, folded under Imported: its own media, and what it took from the library; then Saved. Sounds show their waveform across the row, lit as they play. One highlight color, the timeline's teal, in every pane.

  Sounds and Music hold about 5,400 sound effects and 1,200 pieces of music, each with its own name and group in five languages, every piece of music with a photo cover of its mood.

  Text starts from one line of words whose look and motion are its own to change: basic lines (title, subtitle, body, note, a line below), 36 word-art looks and motions in, while shown and out. Click one to see it; double-click it to put a new line in with it, or onto the words picked. A line's way out is timed by its clip, however long it is trimmed. And 58 text templates, each at its own size: titles, name cards, callouts, shop prices and buttons, explainers, chapters, endings, variety-show words, chat bubbles, news bars, places and dates, holiday greetings; their words and colors are properties in the inspector.

  Stickers hold 1,587 emoji in 3D, Microsoft's Fluent Emoji, named in five languages and grouped (faces, people, animals and nature, food, activities, travel, objects, symbols); each comes in with a pop, at its own size in the middle of the picture. And 45 stickers that move: hand-drawn arrows and marks, emphasis, follow, like and subscribe buttons, badges and decorations.

  Filters are 66 color grades in ten groups (film, cinema, portrait, food, scenery, city, fresh, vintage, black and white, mood), each shown on a photo of its kind. A filter mixes the channels and bends a tone curve per channel, so it can tint the shadows one way and the highlights another, as a grade does; the inspector shows the filter a clip wears with its strength, and the adjustments go on after it.

  Effects hold 70 overlays: grain, light leaks and flares, snow, petals and gold dust, fireflies and bokeh, 8 mm film and a DV camcorder, frames from a phone to a gold frame, variety-show speed lines and marks, dreamy sparkles, confetti, fireworks and balloons. Transitions hold 58 that cover their cut: flashes and light, shapes, motion graphics, glitches, film, comic, smoke and ink, hearts and confetti; besides the timeline's dissolves.

  Templates are short films to make your own: places numbered for your videos and photos (replace each, or drop one on it), with titles, stickers, effects, transitions and music already on them. They lay out for wide and tall films alike.

A group is held by its title bar, as in node editors: a drag there moves it with what it holds, a click picks it, and a drag anywhere inside it draws a selection box over its cards. The bar of list, map, wires, grid and zoom sits at the bottom right; the chat beside the canvas opens and folds from a layout button in the top bar, left of the Canvas button.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Canvas: a project can have several canvases (new, rename, duplicate, delete into the trash, open in a new window), and each can be seen as a storyboard of its material by kind. Generation history lists everything the canvases made, in this project or every project, ready to put back on the board. A character library keeps characters (portrait, full body, expressions, turnaround) for every project: generate one from a few words, keep a group or a picture as one, and wire it into any picture or clip. Style and effects libraries put ready-made words for a look or a camera move on the board, with favorites and recently used. The model list shows how long each model took you and what it takes.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - The canvas works with every service. A making node's model list shows each service's models, the person's own first and the others a click from connecting, each with what it makes (lengths, how many pictures it takes, whether it starts from a first frame). The node then offers only what its model makes: its shapes, lengths and qualities, and references or first and last frames when it takes both. Pictures wired into a picture node are its references, so a character made once stays the same in every shot.

  A service that is not built in can be added as a provider of one's own: a `provider.mjs` in the shared home's `providers/<id>/` folder, following the built-in providers' contract, read again when it changes. It shows in Settings, on the canvas and in `openfilm get`, and agents are told where and how to write one from a service's API docs.

  An empty canvas starts from four ways of working, each saying what it gets: picture to clip, a character (portrait and turnaround), a three-shot storyboard, and a short episode (a character, three shots made with them, their clips cut onto the timeline). Templates leave the words to write, each note saying what to write for what it feeds, and open the first one to type in. A node will not run with an empty note or an unmade node wired in, and a run the service's balance could not pay says so plainly.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Canvas: GPT Image 2.6's own abilities on the board. A picture node can be made as a GIF (its frames and each frame's length) or an SVG; any picture can be changed in part — brush the area to redraw, or draw the change on it in a color that stands out — and the change is made as a new picture beside it. The model list says which models do these. Empty cards are one centered composition, their starts as pills; the settings popovers are lighter, a slider for lengths and frames.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - The canvas looks and works as the node canvases people know: a near-black board with a fine dot grid; cards a step lighter with a grey line (a light one when selected), named above in grey and numbered as they were added ("Picture node 2"); a picture's or a clip's card is its shape at 350 high, and takes a new shape when another is asked. An empty card shows its kind large and what to start with: a new text offers writing in it, or a picture, a clip or music made from it; a clip offers being made from a first frame, or between a first and a last. An empty canvas says to double-click it and offers a picture, a clip, audio, a script (asked of the agent) or a cut.

  The panel under a selected node is wider and rounder, with a button to write in a taller box. A picture node has presets — storyboards, the next moments of a picture, natural skin, cinematic light, a panorama, nine cameras, and character, face, location and product sheets — each a tag before its words that names the node and sets its shape and quality. A clip is made in one of five ways its model makes: from words, everything as reference, from a picture, between first and last frames, or from picture references. "From the canvas" picks what goes in by clicking it on the board. Model lists show each service's mark.

  A selected picture can be seen from nine angles, split into its 2×2 or 3×3 cells, or have its background taken out; a clip gives the frame it shows. A group shows a bar with its color, Make all, keep as a template and ungroup, and its name small above it. Text is written with a bar for headings, bold, italic and a divider. The toolbar at the foot has add, move, templates and the keys (in four columns); the bar at the bottom left has the chat, the list of canvas elements, the overview map, wires shown or hidden, snapping to the grid and the zoom. The selected node's wires are blue and flow. The canvas's right-click menus add uploading, undo, redo and copying a picture to the clipboard; Tab adds a node, ⌘0 fits the canvas and ⌘F finds a node.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Clips move. A clip's style may carry a CSS animation, its `@keyframes` in film.html's `<style>`: it runs on the clip's own time, moves the picture with `translate`, `scale` and `rotate` on top of its box (about its middle), and `--clip-length` times a way out from the clip's end. Agents write motion the way they write any CSS animation.

  In Studio, cutting a clip keeps its motion going in the second half, a copy gets keyframes of its own, and every change undoes.

  The library adds 27 clip animations (12 ways in, 8 ways out, 7 loops, such as pop, whip, spin, blur, shake, heartbeat and a slow push) on the picked clips, and 11 camera moves on a cut (zoom through, pull back, whips in four directions, spins, flash zoom, blur, shake), the clip before the cut going out as the one after it comes in.

Its picture shows as a page's, and its sound is heard in the film that holds it, each sound where the clip plays that part of it, at its volume times the clip's, the clip's fades on the ones at its edges. Exports and Studio's preview hear it too.

  In Studio, ⌥G (or New compound clip in a clip's menu) makes the picked clips one compound clip: their own film in `parts/<name>/film.html`, one clip where they were, undone like any edit. ⌥⇧G (Break apart compound clip) puts its clips back where it plays them, cut to the part it plays. A double-click opens a compound clip's film to edit, with the project's media, and the timeline's toolbar leads back (‹ project / name); the film around it shows the change at once.

It makes clips with their own sound, 3 to 15 seconds long (10 at most between a first and a last frame), wide or tall, 360p to 4K. A clip starts from a prompt, a first frame (and optionally a last one), or up to 6 reference pictures. Veo is no longer offered.

  Gemini Omni also takes a clip from the project (`openfilm get video --video`), with a Google key or through OpenRouter. It edits the clip as the prompt says, or continues it when the prompt asks. `--duration` gives the 3 to 10 seconds a continuation adds; without it, the clip keeps its own length. A clip can be at most 15 s long to edit, and at most 30 s in all once continued. A made clip's length and size are read from the file that comes back.

Open Projects immediately on a title click, keep renaming in place from its actions, and label untitled conversations as New chat.

  Refresh the project hover cover after setting a frame or picture as its cover. Remove repeated action tooltips and conversation headings, make New chat easier to find, and keep skill management in Community instead of Agents settings.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Every skill is known by its full name, `owner/name`: `openfilm/replicate` for OpenFilm's own, `webgrs/borrow-style` for a community skill, `local/<name>` for your own and `project/<name>` for a project's. Agents always get a skill by that full name, so two skills with the same name never get mixed up and the name doesn't change from one session to the next. Claude Code gets one plugin per owner (`openfilm:replicate`, `webgrs:borrow-style`, `local:my-brand`); other agents get `openfilm-replicate`, and each skill's text opens with its full name and who made it. `openfilm skill` takes a full name; a bare name works only when one skill has it, and otherwise every match is listed by its full name. `openfilm skills` says whether an installed skill is OpenFilm's or the community's. A GitHub account named `local` or `project` becomes `github-local` or `github-project`.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Every skill has a uid, whose it is and its name: `openfilm/keynote` for OpenFilm's, `river/keynote` for one added from River's GitHub (`gitlab-<group>/…` from GitLab), `local/…` for one made here or added from a folder, `project/…` for a project's own. The owner is read from where the skill came from, never from what it says of itself, so none can pass for OpenFilm's. Names can repeat: two skills of a name are had side by side, each turned on and off, opened and removed by its uid, and the same skill added again replaces only itself. An agent, which reads skills by name alone, is given the first of a name by it (the project's, then the person's, then the official one) and the others as `owner-name`. OpenFilm's own skills are `openfilm/edit`, `openfilm/media` and `openfilm/canvas`. `openfilm skills` lists skills by uid and `openfilm skill <owner/name>` prints one. In Studio's library each installed skill is a line with its uid under its title, in parts headed with their counts, Import beside the search; a search finds a skill by its uid too.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Discover sorts skills by who made them: All, OpenFilm or Community. A community skill shows its maker's name on its cover and on its page ("Community · by WEBGRS", linking to their account). It comes after OpenFilm's own, in the order the index lists it. A skill's page says what it needs beyond OpenFilm and which computers it runs on, and a skill that can't run on this computer says so and can't be installed. The page also counts the scripts a skill brings, which run on your computer, and marks a skill added from a link as not reviewed by OpenFilm. Installed skills are grouped as OpenFilm's and the community's. `openfilm.json` gains `needs` and `platforms`.

OpenFilm's system skills, `edit`, `media` and `canvas`, are listed there with a title and a summary in each language, and each can be turned off: one turned off is given to no agent, and the desktop app's own agent is no longer told to read `edit` when it is off. Agents are given them in that order, edit, media, canvas.
