The subtitles Studio drew over the picture from the voices' transcripts, with their own row, switch, style panel and line list, are gone; a project that showed them has them made its caption track as it opens, once, as they looked and where they sat, and is told so. The transcripts beside the media stay as they are.

  - A caption on the picture is selected with a click (⇧/⌘-click one more or one less) and moved with a drag: every caption of the track with "Apply to all captions" on, the selected ones alone with it off. Its middle snaps to the picture's middle.
  - A caption track is a CC track: its head has the CC mark, its row is narrow and each caption shows its words. A caption is a line of words as a text is: picked on the picture it has the frame every picked thing has (its middle moves it, a corner scales its words, a side makes it wider or narrower), and its Inspector is a text's, the same tabs (Text, Word art, Motion) with the library's word art and motions, under "All captions" (or the picked ones alone). A Captions tab has what only captions have: every line to read and correct, the one playing lit, where they sit, their words lit as said, translation into another caption track, deleting the picked. ⌘L refers the selected captions.
  - Caption tracks are made in the library's Captions: of what is said in the film, from an SRT or WebVTT file, or new with one caption at the playhead (picked, its words to type in the Inspector); each is a new caption track on top. A caption track's eye shows or hides it, as any track's does; the timeline has no CC button.
  - A row holding only the library's texts (name cards, callouts, titles…) is drawn as a caption track is: narrow, each clip by its words. A text of the library dropped on a row of another kind gets a row of text of its own, just above it, and a text put in without a box of its own gets its shape's box rather than covering the whole picture.
  - Tracks are numbered as editors number them: V1, V2… for picture, counted from the bottom up (pages and caption tracks are picture too, so there is no P or T), and A1, A2… for sound; a track head shows its number and no name. A caption track is dragged up or down by its head as any track is, its captions drawn over the tracks below it.
  - The editing skill says what Studio makes of a voice's words: a caption track, when the person asks for captions.
  - Exports draw caption tracks as part of the picture: where a film has them, the "burn in" switch gives way to a note that a hidden track is left out. `openfilm render --subtitles` renders a film with caption tracks as it is, and still burns in the subtitles of a project not opened since.

With no agent ready, a service connected for translating does it; outside the app, that service does it as before.

ElevenLabs speaks with Eleven v4 and Eleven v4 Turbo.

### Patch Changes

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Settings → Services: OpenFilm, once signed in, has View account, opening your account on the website.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Adding a file or a library sound or music with "+" or Enter puts it in at the playhead, and the playhead stays where it is. Only the source viewer's insert and overwrite move the playhead to the end of what they put in.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Add (`plus-lighter`) is among the blend modes: light added to what is under it, as overlays of tape noise and flares are composited.

- [#338](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/338) [`2e18a4b`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/2e18a4bdf54647b2ece3c0975a6f9573720c7821) Thanks [@yunloong-Y](https://github.com/yunloong-Y)! - Desktop: the Community's "Use it" and "make one with the agent" now close it, putting the person at the conversation. A skill only added keeps the Skills page open, where the person sees it.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Studio: while an agent works in the project, what it is changing is not shown as broken. The picture drawn last stays, dimmed and said to be updating, when the film cannot be drawn; a clip whose file is gone waits, gray and striped, and the viewer does not say it is missing. Once the agent is done, a film that still cannot be drawn says so in a sentence, the film's own words under Details. `edit` tells agents to take a file out of film.html before they delete it.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - In the dark theme what is picked or read is brighter and what is only there steps back, in Studio and the chat: brighter text, dimmer secondary words, a brighter picked row and picked track. A track's head reads in two parts: what the row holds, a dim mark on the left, then its switches (lock, hide, mute, solo) in their own columns, brighter at rest and the accent when on.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Exports and Studio never fail with `ERR_UNSAFE_PORT`: a local server is no longer given a port browsers refuse to open (such as 3659, which some Windows computers hand out). When it gets one, it asks for another.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A film page that draws a 2D canvas stroke by stroke through `ctx.filter = 'blur(…)'` no longer freezes the computer. On a Mac, Chromium's Skia Graphite GPU path took 7 to 9 GB for one such frame. Thumbnails and exports draw without Graphite. The Mac desktop preview submits smaller batches to the GPU, keeping both 2D Canvas and WebGL accelerated in films such as One Prompt. Each browser OpenFilm starts to draw pages is also watched, and ended when one of its processes holds more than a quarter of the machine's memory; such a page is not drawn again until it changes, and an export of it fails saying why instead of starting again. A thumbnail browser that crashed is replaced at once. When the app's Studio stops again and again right after starting, it is started again later each time, up to a minute apart, instead of every second.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Canvas: the board is the panels' grey (rgb 24) like every pane's inside, a group a step lighter, and the line under the top bar (and by the chat) runs over the canvas too.

- [#363](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/363) [`94ea960`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/94ea960d53f55fb34c80745dffb65830c0124afb) Thanks [@WEBGRS](https://github.com/WEBGRS)! - Studio: when the canvas changes shape, a video or still that filled it without a box of its own gets a box in its picture's shape, where the picture already was. Its box used to stay the whole new canvas, bars and all, so on a 16:9 video made 9:16, fading its top and bottom edges faded the black bars and the picture showed no fade; a crop, a mask and round corners missed the picture the same way. A picture cropped or stretched to the canvas (`object-fit: cover` or `fill`) still fills it, without a box.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Canvas: everything on the board and over it (cards, groups, menus, panels, its bars and windows) takes the panels' grey (rgb 24) on the window's (rgb 20). The storyboard view is gone: the cut node puts the clips on the timeline in order, and a group's Make all makes what is missing.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Canvas: every way a model makes a clip can be picked, each saying what it takes; picked, its first (and last) frame show as slots to fill, and a clip from a first frame takes its shape. The storyboard is now the film's shot list in the cut's order: make what is missing, then put every clip on the timeline in order. What an agent lays out on the canvas opens it and comes into view. Nodes made at the same moment no longer share a file. The top left, menus, panels and windows are lighter and come in smoothly.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Canvas: going from the edit to the canvas and back is seamless. Once opened, the canvas stays as it was left (its view, what is selected, what is open), hidden under the edit, instead of loading and fitting its board again each time; the first time it shows, it shows already fitted. ⌘E (Ctrl+E) switches between the edit and the canvas from either, set in Settings › Keyboard shortcuts with the others. The shortcuts panel sits in the middle of the board's foot, with the switch in it. The list of what is on the board filters by kind (pictures, videos, sounds, text, groups) as well as by words. A made picture carries no "AI" tag, and a making node says "Inputs changed" only when what is wired into it changed since it made one, not when its own words or settings did.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Canvas: Chinese and other input methods write in a text node and a node's prompt as typed (the box no longer drops what is being composed, leaving pinyin behind). A text node is moved by dragging anywhere on it; a double click, Enter or simply typing while it is picked writes in it. Its words are drawn as Markdown: headings, bulleted, numbered and to-do lists, quotes, code, bold, italic, strikethrough and rules, with a list button in its bar. The redraw dialog has its own keys (⌘Z / ⇧⌘Z, [ ], E, B) and the board under it takes none. The board's dots are visible at every zoom, Beta sits at its bottom left, and nodes are listed in the order a film is made: text, image, audio, video, cut.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Studio: a caption's inspector is Captions, Text and Animation. Captions shows the track at a glance and every line numbered, to search, correct in place, add a caption after or delete. Cut by captions ticks lines to cut out of the film with their picture and sound, everything after closed up, and Translate puts the track in another language just above it. Text › Basic holds the caption's words, font, size, bold, underline, italic, color, letter and line spacing, alignment, edge, box, shadow, place and word lighting. Below them are preset looks, each drawn as a "T", and Save preset keeps a look of one's own. A look goes on all captions or on the ones picked.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Caption tracks: a track in film.html can hold one `<track kind="captions" src="captions/….vtt">`, a standard WebVTT file on the film's time, and the film draws its cues over the tracks below it. The look is the CSS of the file's STYLE blocks: `::cue` for the whole track, `::cue(#c3)` for one cue, `<c.em>` spans, voices, and word timestamps with `:past` / `:future`. Any CSS property works there, animations included. Cue settings and REGION place the cues. A cue's words also take the library text's CSS variables (`--font`, `--size`, `--fill`, `--stroke`, `--glow`, `--box`, `--in`, `--loop`, `--out`…), its fonts and motions included, as a text does. Preview, `look` and exports all draw them, and SRT/VTT files are written from them.

  In Studio, on the timeline, a caption track is a narrow text row, each cue a block with a caption mark before its words, and a clip like any other: it is selected, boxed with other clips, dragged (to another caption track too), trimmed, split with ⌘B, copied, cut and pasted (its look with it), deleted, rippled and carried by linkage by the same gestures and edits, and one undo takes back what an edit did to clips and captions together. Nothing but captions goes onto a caption track, and captions go onto nothing else. A caption is added from the row's menu, its words typed in the inspector (not past the film's end). The inspector changes the look of all captions, or with "Apply to all captions" unticked only the selected ones: size, weight, color, outline, background and height on the picture. It says when a selected cue has a look of its own, and puts it back to the track's. Every edit is one undo step, and a file the agent changes shows at once.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Studio: the library's Captions are laid out as its other categories are, right after Skills. New holds three cards: captions from speech, which says how much is said and makes them again after cuts; import subtitles; and a new caption at the playhead. Translate holds a card for each language, which puts the captions translated into it on a track just above them and says the track a language is on already. Each card is done with a click or its "+". The media drawer is framed as the chat's column is, with a line of the app's ground at its top.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Studio: the captions of what is said make themselves. Once a voice with a transcript is in the film (a voice made with its words timed has one), a caption track of it is put on top, with nothing transcribed again. While no one changes those captions by hand they follow the film: a voice moved, trimmed or put in, and they are written again in their look. Captions changed by hand are left as they are, and "Make again" in the library's Captions makes them follow again. A caption track of speech the person took out is not put back.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Studio: the Layers panel lists the captions shown at the playhead with the clips, each where its caption track is drawn; a caption's row picks it, as a click on it on the picture does.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Studio: the library's New captions makes caption tracks of what the film says or of a subtitle file. A sound that already has its words, as a voice-over has, is used as it is, with nothing to choose. Only the sounds that need recognizing are ticked, in the list or at a clip's top right on the timeline, where the clips the captions are made of are framed while the page is open. What to make is ticked too: the captions in the words said, their translation into a language on a track of its own, or the translation alone. A line under the button says what will happen. A clip whose words the captions come from shows a CC mark before its name on the timeline.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Studio: the library's Captions are one page. It says how much is said in the film, which clips have no transcript yet, and which caption track the captions are on. Making them again puts what is said now, after the cuts, in place of that track's captions, their look kept. Clips that cannot be transcribed (no service) no longer stop the rest from being made captions. A subtitle file (SRT or WebVTT) dropped on the timeline or the picture becomes a caption track from where it lands, and "Add a caption" puts one at the playhead on the top caption track. Words with their times keep the line's own spaces ("Comfortable, right?").

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Studio: a caption on the picture is picked, framed and dragged as anything there is, with the plain pointer, and is shown whole while dragged out of its region. ⌘Z undoes a change made with a button or slider in the inspector right away. The caption inspector's Cut by transcript shows the track's words as text: words picked with a drag and Delete cut their picture and sound out of the film, everything after closed up. Animation starts with Word by word, a tile for each effect (color, appear, fade, rise, pop, blur, grow, bounce, flash, box, underline), each playing under the pointer, with the color it lights with; captions with no word times can have them estimated by their length, and a translation of words timed as said has its own words timed too.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A page's poster in the media drawer is drawn with what the page leaves clear kept transparent, on a checkerboard, as its preview is. When the library's categories do not all fit, a button at the bar's end shows the last ones and then goes back to the first, for a mouse without a sideways wheel.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - What is picked stands out: a brighter accent, and every picked choice (the library's category with a bar under it, its side list's line, the inspector's category and its segmented choices) on the accent's own tint instead of a grey. The viewer's pane is a step darker than the panels around it. A double click or "+" on a text style or a preset of one's own puts in a new line of words in it, never changes the words picked; a look goes on them from the Text inspector's word art.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A clip's animation is set in the inspector's Animation: In, Out and Loop, a dot on each that has one, the library's motions on square cards drawn on the clip's own picture (standing mid-move, playing under the pointer), None first, and how long the one on takes (In and Out fit in the clip together). A motion put on plays on the picture. Videos, pictures, stickers and other pages take one; the library's effects, filters, transitions, words and sounds do not. The effects drawer no longer lists them; camera moves on a cut stay among the transitions.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Timeline: with linkage on, deleting a main-track clip no longer deletes the sounds that ride on it. Titles and overlays over it still go with it, but a voice, a sound effect or music stays where it is.

- [#360](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/360) [`3e6b217`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/3e6b217a72ec4e03cbd3f2943d6f1cf3004408d6) Thanks [@WEBGRS](https://github.com/WEBGRS)! - Studio: Detach audio works on several selected videos at once. With more than one video selected the clip menu had no Detach audio and Ctrl+Shift+S took the sound off only the first; now every selected video with sound is made silent and gets its own linked sound, in one step to undo. Sounds that play at the same time go on different sound tracks.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Settings: Developer no longer has a Release group; this version's notes are shown again from General.

- [#355](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/355) [`5702bf4`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/5702bf4fd9dba58e0cc81ca9d97761641b1c92f7) Thanks [@yunloong-Y](https://github.com/yunloong-Y)! - `openfilm doctor` now says how much the projects' hidden `.film` folders hold (render cache, version history and logs), so the gigabytes a long film's seek copies take are visible instead of unexplained. The folders are read asynchronously, so Studio's Settings → Developer → Copy report and its diagnostics file do not hold the app while a network drive or a cold disk is walked.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - 剪映 and CapCut drafts: footage or a still drawn with a look of its own (a crop, a fading mask, round corners) and no picture under it is written opaque on the film's background as H.264, as the film shows it, instead of a lossless PNG of every frame. A film of 30 such clips went from 4.2 GB to 1.6 GB, its footage from 2.7 GB to 140 MB.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A thing dragged from the library or the media pane and let go back over them ends at once, instead of first flying back to where the drag began.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - The drawer's player runs down under the composer with no strip between them. A voice row fades into its own color under its buttons, only while the pointer is on it. A page picked in the media drawer is previewed on a checkerboard, so what it leaves clear is seen to be clear.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - In the desktop app the media drawer runs down under the chat's composer, which floats over it with its own shadow. A voice's sample plays in the drawer's player, as sounds and music do. Every library card's star and "+" are round buttons of one size, and a voice row's end gets a deeper shade so they stand out. The featured mark on a voice is solid, and the drawer's saved media is called Favorites.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Studio: the top bar's switch between the edit and the canvas shows the one open, its icon and name; a click goes to the other.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - The dark theme's greys are two: the panels rgb(24, 24, 24), the window's ground, its top bars, the viewer and the canvas rgb(20, 20, 20). What is picked is in cyan (#00c1cd): a category of the library's top bar by its color alone, with no bar or fill; a line of its side list in cyan, its headings grey boxes.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A filter put in with a double click or "+" goes on a track of its own from the playhead, a clip that grades every layer under it while it is on (its strength in the inspector); dropped on a clip, it grades that clip alone. Clicking a filter tries it on the whole picture. Filters, effects, transitions and templates show on square cards; a filter's card is the film's own picture at the playhead in that filter, an effect's card its page laid over that picture. Filter and effect clips have colors and marks of their own on the timeline.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - The first clip put on an empty timeline stays where it was let go: the timeline keeps the scale it had while empty (the widest view is now never less than that), instead of zooming to the new film.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - With the main track's magnet off, the first clip dropped on an empty timeline lands where it is let go, not at the start.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Pictures from OpenAI are made with GPT Image 2.6 Flare by default, or 2.6 Sunburst; GPT Image 2.5 Flare and Sunburst stay, and GPT Image 2 is gone (a film set to it uses 2.6 Flare). `openfilm get image` takes `--markup`, a transparent PNG drawn on to say what of the first picture to change; `--format gif`, a looping animation of `--frames` (2 to 16) of `--frame-ms` each, its frames as large as GPT Image makes them in the shape asked; and `--format svg`, a vector picture from the prompt alone, its cost worked out with the writing's. A GIF, or an animated PNG or WebP, in a film now plays by the film's time: the frame its own timing has at the clip's time, from the clip's start, looping as the file says, the same in Studio, `look` and an export.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A clip draws nothing outside its time: a mask's outline, or any other `filter`, no longer shows on the picture before the clip starts or after it ends.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A clip made larger, smaller or turned on the picture is pointed at, hovered and clicked where it is drawn, not where it would be unscaled: what the picture finds under the pointer now counts the clip's transform about its middle, as the film draws it.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - The film inspector's Advanced tab offers motion blur, samples per frame, and shutter for video exports. A page clip can put its own controls in Advanced when it declares them as component properties.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A provider key that reached the spending limit set on it (OpenRouter's "Key limit exceeded") is no longer reported as a key that wasn't accepted. The run stops and says the key's limit is what to change: raise or remove it where the key was made, or use another key. As with a used-up balance, nothing is retried.

- [#364](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/364) [`6a46337`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/6a463379aa89a8585af9894d89ec3d52320c95f3) Thanks [@WEBGRS](https://github.com/WEBGRS)! - Studio: a text color typed into its field, then a stroke added with the next click, keeps the color. Leaving the field and clicking Add stroke made the two edits at once, and the stroke was worked out from the film as it was before the color, so writing it took the color away again: the words went back to the color they had. The same held for any two changes to a layer made that close together.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Layers shows each clip by its kind (words, a sticker, an effect, a video, a still) and pointing at a clip's row outlines it on the picture; the font list picks only fonts it lists; a drag onto the timeline is previewed as it lands, as tall as the row and in the look it will have.

- [#332](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/332) [`1ca1a64`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/1ca1a64c77bc190bbd7f9882a2f462040431f103) Thanks [@rick-mingyu-liu](https://github.com/rick-mingyu-liu)! - Studio's Layers panel lists a page that took a moment to draw. A page coming on is in the picture only once its first frame is drawn; the panel had read the picture before that (a slow page, a slow computer) and not again, so the page was missing from the list until the playhead moved. The picture now tells Studio when a page it drew came on, and the panel reads again.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - The library's categories can be ordered and turned off in Settings › Advanced (the media always first and shown), and templates come right after the skills. A track of the film's videos and pictures is drawn taller than one of stickers, effects or text; a sticker or text goes into the main track's gap when that track holds no film of its own. The lines between the panes show again.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A sticker or a line of words is moved by pressing and dragging it, picked or not; after its words are typed it stays whole; its start can be pulled earlier and trimming it keeps its way in; resetting its place puts it in the middle at its size; Crop on the picture is offered only for videos and stills; the inspector no longer tells how to make a property of a sticker's layer.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A sticker, effect or title of the library is one thing on the picture: pointed at, pressed or dragged it is the whole clip, never a part of its page moved out of its box and cut by it, and the Layers panel shows it as one row. A sticker on the timeline is a green block with its picture at its start.

- [#358](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/358) [`bafa7aa`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bafa7aa8964e5bc50b39c893f6ccf552f0ad400f) Thanks [@rick-mingyu-liu](https://github.com/rick-mingyu-liu)! - Moving the library to another disk no longer counts a project as not moved when the trash will not take its old folder (on Windows, the Recycle Bin refusing one bigger than it holds): the copy on the new disk is whole, so it is the project from then on, and Studio says which old folders stay on the old disk, for the file manager to delete. Before, the person was told the move failed while a full copy sat in the new library unlisted.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Every text template is put in at its own size: the full-picture ones (titles, lower thirds, badges, the countdown, the progress bar, the subtitle box) now draw in a box of their own, in the middle of the picture or near its foot. Every text template and every sticker with words has a Font property, so its font is changed with the font picker in the inspector, and a template's Text field shows its own words. The stickers are listed as Favorites, Animated stickers and Still stickers; the library's side list shows each heading as a box and the line shown in the accent, as its category bar does.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - `openfilm get sfx-search` and `music-search` say what of the prompt each match has and lacks, and which the film already uses, so the agent chooses between the library and making one as the film needs; the manual says so, and Settings → Services has a Library search row: OpenFilm's vector search, free with no sign-in, on by default, or None for words alone. "cut" no longer finds cute sounds.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Searching OpenFilm's library is quicker: about a tenth of a second with Studio running (it keeps the library loaded), and well under half a second without, where it took a second.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Library: skills come second, as covers, with what is installed and what is still to install, each tried or installed from its cover and opened on a page of its own. An example can be copied into a new project to change, or, over a film, handed to its chat for the agent to follow.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Library: a list that mixes OpenFilm's own things with the community's (sent to the library, each with who sent it) or the person's own has chips under its search to show one source, and a thing not OpenFilm's own wears its source on its corner. Skills show theirs the same way. The examples' dialog can open on the examples to start from, with copying one and letting the agent follow it side by side.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Studio's media drawer lists only the files brought into the project under Imported. Things taken from the library, tried once or kept in the film, stay in their own category instead of piling up in a "From the library" list.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Settings → Services: Local services, last before adding a service, are the models that run on this computer, free and offline. Each shows whether it is installed and whether it is on, and its page tells who made it, its size, memory and license, and where it is used, with Install, a switch and Uninstall.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Local services transcribe and take backgrounds out on this computer, free and offline. Whisper Small or Whisper Large v3 Turbo writes down what a recording says, each word timed (each character, in Chinese and Japanese), in the language it hears; BiRefNet Lite makes a picture's background transparent, with `openfilm get cutout`. Installed, a model makes its kind when nothing else was chosen for it, and appears among that kind's choices in Settings → Services.

- [#334](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/334) [`df83773`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/df837734056146968fcdea96edef081c615eccf6) Thanks [@WEBGRS](https://github.com/WEBGRS)! - `look --full` watches every frame of the rendered film (or of a video given to `look`) for what a dozen frames miss: white flashes, frames unlike those on both sides, still pictures of 6 seconds or more, and black, each with its time. One ffmpeg decode at two small sizes; a 2-minute film takes a few seconds.

- [#354](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/354) [`6138a37`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/6138a376888615909b4cfa475027e059983422e0) Thanks [@yunloong-Y](https://github.com/yunloong-Y)! - `look`: a contact sheet now draws both ends of the film or range, so a page that changes only near its end is no longer left out of the check that draws each frame twice. A sheet of a media file stops two frames short of the file's end, where ffmpeg can still take a picture.

- [#333](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/333) [`ab5b108`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/ab5b1080384da522c84cc43a985cec2811fafa2f) Thanks [@WEBGRS](https://github.com/WEBGRS)! - Loudness: `render --loudness=<LUFS>` and a Loudness choice in Studio's video export bring the film's sound to a delivery target (-14 for streaming, -16 for podcasts, -23 for broadcast), its true peaks under -1 dBTP. The mix is measured, raised or lowered as a whole, and its few loudest peaks are caught by a limiter working at four times the sample rate, then measured again as encoded; the picture is not touched. `look --loudness=<LUFS>` says how far the mix is from a target. Without the option, nothing changes.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Manual: shots that follow one another go end to end on one track. A transition at a cut is one of the library's, put over the cut, or each shot's own way in and out on that track. Only two clips that must show at once overlap on two tracks; before, every transition was taught as two clips overlapping on two tracks, which split a film's shots across two tracks taking turns.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Studio: the timeline's markers float above the rows instead of taking a row of their own, so adding the first marker or removing the last one leaves the tracks where they are. The canvas's "Ask your agent to lay it out" shows a send-to-chat icon.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Studio: a master-quality export keeps a dark, smooth gradient smooth (#366). Its frames are taken as PNG instead of JPEG, and footage the browser needs a copy of is copied near the file (x264 at a high quality, its grain kept) instead of on the graphics chip at a bitrate, in a copy of its own beside the preview's; HEVC at master is written in 10 bits (Main 10). A Blender render's dithered glow came out in hard bands and now keeps its grain. A master export takes a little longer: about a tenth more at 1080p, a fifth at 4K.

- [#369](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/369) [`3eacfd6`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/3eacfd63ff841c11712f7b0ac5699939fa141d96) Thanks [@rick-mingyu-liu](https://github.com/rick-mingyu-liu)! - Studio: once a write of film.html that kept the person's edits is in place, Studio knows it at once. It no longer waits first on removing the temporary copy (a slow moment on Windows), during which an agent's command could read the merged film and not yet be told what was kept.

- [#341](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/341) [`bf46b5f`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bf46b5f006b042f7c34ec316a4ed6f684549e992) Thanks [@rick-mingyu-liu](https://github.com/rick-mingyu-liu)! - A film's sound mix ended with its last sound at times, not at the film's end (ffmpeg 8 on Linux, about one mix in 25 when the sounds are files of their own): the mixed frames came without timestamps, and the padding to the film's length goes by them. The mix is given its timestamps, from its samples, before it is padded.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - The chat's notices are one kind of card on the composer, rising in, each with its mark (information, warning, error), its words, what to do about it and a close button where it can be left: an agent that cannot be used any more, a turn that failed (with Copy diagnostics, Switch account), a balance run out, a message waiting for its turn, the context almost full, and Studio's own trouble (ffmpeg, its browser), which was a strip over the chat. Undoing a turn is asked on the composer too, not in a dialog over the window. Studio's installed skills are listed in this order: All, Imported, System, Community, Agents' own.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Pictures, videos and pages that are clear in places show it the same way everywhere, on a faint checkerboard in either theme: a picture's or a video's poster with an alpha is kept as a PNG, and the preview lays them out at their own shape. Something dragged from the library or the media drawer becomes the clip it will be as soon as it is over the timeline, solid where it lands, its picture no longer following the pointer there. The drawer's player floats as a card above the composer, and a heading in the drawer's side bar that folds lines of its own has a light fill.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A line of words from the library goes in with a box that hugs the words, and on the picture it is one thing: pointed at, hovered, pressed or dragged, it is its whole clip with one frame, and a double-click still goes in to type. The line's ways of coming in, moving and going out stay within the room its box keeps around the words, so they are never cut by the box.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Studio and the app's chat have one palette, light and dark, on a cool gray (a soft charcoal in dark). Backgrounds step up plainly from the window to the panes to what is raised over them (cards, menus, dialogs), with fields, hover, pressed and selected the same on every layer; text has three levels and lines two. The main actions are ink, near black in light and near white in dark; one teal accent marks what is selected, current or focused: progress, the timeline's toggles and the frame on the picture, with switches turned on a step quieter. The tab or the place picked among others (the inspector's tabs, the library's categories and its rail) is the accent on a raised fill. On the timeline, each track is a square band of its own from its head across, a quiet step off the panel under it and apart from the next by a narrow gap of that panel; its clips are each a quiet color by kind (video, image, page, text and captions, and one for every sound) under white words, with its waveform drawn lighter over it; the playhead and a picked clip's frame are white (ink in light), the track of a picked clip is a shade brighter, and a picked gap between clips is not drawn (its menu works as before). A track's sound switch is a pair of headphones, and solo is a plain S; the timeline's switches go snapping, the main-track magnet, then linkage. A button that cannot be used now is plainly dimmer than one that can, and the panes' top bars are 40 px tall, level with the tabs beside them.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - At OpenRouter, Seedance fetches a voice recording (`--voice`) or a reference clip only from a web address. A run that can make a temporary address sends the file by that address; OpenFilm's own service is one such run. With your own key OpenFilm has nowhere to put the file, so this can't be done there: the run says so before anything is sent and points to fal, which takes the file straight from the project. Studio's OpenRouter card says the same. Anyone who needs it with their own key can add their own storage in `providers/openrouter.mjs`, for themselves or in a pull request. Previously OpenRouter refused the request with "Only HTTPS URLs are allowed".

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - At OpenRouter, Seedance takes a clip as a reference (`openfilm get video --video`), alongside its pictures and voice. The clip must be 2 s up to the model's longest length. It is sent by a temporary web address, which OpenFilm's own service gives it; with your own key this can't be done, and the run says why. A first frame given with a reference clip is used as one of the reference pictures, and a last frame is left out. Gemini Omni still edits or continues a clip.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Studio: a clip swapped by a script or an agent outside Studio, and then swapped back, stays as written. The swap back read exactly like a version Studio had seen before, so it was taken for an old copy written again, and about 0.3 s later Studio put the clip back as the first swap had made it, without a word, until the app was restarted. Only what the person changed in Studio is kept over a write now; what came from another write is the latest write's. (#344)

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A template asks for your own videos and pictures before it goes in: its places numbered in the order they play, with their lengths, your project's videos and pictures to click into them, From this computer, and Fill; Use template puts it in with them, no empty places left. Filters, effects, transitions and templates in the library show on your own picture (the film's at the playhead, else your first video or picture), or a plain one when you have none, never a stock sample: transitions dissolve, dip, move and wipe on it, a template's title is drawn on it, and trying one in the viewer plays it on the film itself.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Studio no longer says a change from outside undid your edits when you only moved something quickly. The folder told of Studio's own write before Studio heard back from it, and reading the film then showed it without the edits still on their way. The folder's news is now judged once Studio's own writes are answered.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Studio: only the window's ground is the darker grey (rgb 20); every pane's inside is the panels' (rgb 24), the viewer round the picture and the library's top bar among them. In the app, the line under the top bar runs over the chat too.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Paste attributes (⌘⇧V) can carry a clip's mask and its stroke too. The mask is drawn again in each clip's box, and the clip's own faded edges stay.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Timeline: with the magnet on, a clip pasted onto the main track goes into the main track at the cut nearest the playhead, and what follows moves on, as an ⌥-drag copy does. It no longer goes onto a new track above.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Studio: dragging a layer on the picture of a heavy page keeps its frame with the picture: each step is answered once the page has drawn it, so the frame no longer runs ahead while the picture stands still. Bring to front / Send to back on a whole clip moves only that clip (onto a new top or bottom track when its track holds others), not its whole track and the clips beside it. Undoing a track reorder after the agent added or moved tracks puts back the track that moved, in its place. [ and ] pressed in the timeline select left / right of the playhead, even with a layer in hand.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - `openfilm get image` takes every quality GPT Image 2.5 has (`--quality low|medium|high|xhigh|max`, low by default), a size (`--size 1k|2k|4k`), 21:9, up to 16 pictures to make one from (`--ref`), and a mask that redraws part of the first (`--mask`, OpenAI's own). OpenAI and OpenRouter send all of it as each takes it; Gemini and FLUX take the size and say what they leave out. OpenRouter offers GPT Image 2.6 Flare and Sunburst first, then GPT Image 2.5 Flare and Sunburst, and Seedance 2.0 Mini, Wan 3.0, Wan 3.0 Prime, MiniMax Hailuo 3, MiniMax Hailuo 3 Max and HeyGen Video for video, each clip asked for the size its model makes; `get video --duration` goes down to 2 seconds, each model making its shortest at the least. A run of the OpenFilm service is asked about soon after it begins, and a picture's cost is worked out from the tokens OpenAI billed.

- [#322](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/322) [`09e18d7`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/09e18d7089aa4f51445f23b0708ac3d95a1077c4) Thanks [@WEBGRS](https://github.com/WEBGRS)! - Exports: on Windows and Linux, a ProRes 4444 video's likely size now reaches 1.6 times ProRes's rate, as ffmpeg's encoder there spends up to that on detailed footage. On a Mac, which encodes ProRes on its media engine, the range is as before.

- [#342](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/342) [`623cec4`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/623cec4297aa6c134d1426c796e79f5845724433) Thanks [@WEBGRS](https://github.com/WEBGRS)! - Providers: Settings › Providers links each service's own pricing page, as "Pricing" beside what it makes.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Studio: a voice in the library's Voices has Generate beside its star and "+". It opens a box to type words in, which are spoken in that voice and put on a sound track at the playhead, their words timed so their captions come by themselves. Its "?" shows the marks the voice's service reads for pauses, tone and sounds, and a click puts one in at the cursor. The timeline's record button only records.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - The chat folds only the steps that finished; a step still running stands under them with its clock, and joins the count once it is done. The manual and OpenFilm's own skills name the command as `openfilm`, which is the app's own where an agent reads a skill from its file, and the command as it runs when printed (`npx -y openfilm@latest` through npx).

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A page's animations stay held while the film stands still after its overrides change (an undo, a trim, a look put on): they no longer play on by themselves; a page opened after the still frame was drawn shows without moving the playhead.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A sound, video or picture clip kept in Favorites plays in the drawer and shows its waveform or picture, as the file it holds. A file put on the timeline from Favorites or the library is never shown missing for a moment while it is being brought in. The drawer's player sits on the drawer's own ground, lined up with the chat's composer under it. The skills side bar lists Installed, then Favorites, then Discover, and opens on Installed.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - The manual no longer tells agents to render: they check with `openfilm look`, and the person exports from Studio. The desktop app's own agent gets a shorter prompt that says each thing once, without the manual's `open`, since Studio is already open beside it. A reply made on the person's own key shows the key icon from the agent menu.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - The frame of a clip in hand marks the sides it can be pulled by: all four for a video or a picture, the left and right for a line of words. A line of words pulled wider or narrower lays itself out again at its new width as it goes (a long line wraps), instead of being stretched.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Skills: Discover shows the openfilm/skills index as it is now on GitHub, read as Studio starts and again behind it each time it opens, and no longer a copy that came with OpenFilm, so a skill added there shows and one taken away is gone; while it is first read, its tiles' places show, never an empty page. Without a connection it shows what it last read, and says so. An empty conversation's cards are Discover's skills too, and one can be tried there before it is installed. Installing a skill downloads it at the version Discover shows, and it stays that version, offline too; when a newer one is out, the skill says so and Update brings it, the old one going to the trash. A skill taken out of Discover stays installed and working, marked as no longer there. The ten starter skills are retired. `openfilm skills` lists the installed ones as installed.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - The library's skills fold by part (yours, system, community, each agent's own), and any skill can be starred into the side bar's Favorites. A skill to discover shows a star and a "+" over its cover: the "+" names it in the chat's message, and Install is on its page. In the desktop app the chat's composer over the library looks as it does over the chat, without a shadow darkening the library around it.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Studio's installed skills: the person's own head All, shown with none too (where Import's go), and are no longer a line of their own; each agent's own skills are under that agent's name. A skill's page has Back where the search was, at its height. OpenFilm's system skills say on their page when they are used, what they teach agents, and what turning one off means, in each language.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Studio's skills are Installed and Discover. Installed lists the person's own, OpenFilm's system skills, the community's and each agent's own in parts, each skill its title and what it is for (its uid on its page); with none of their own or none of the community's, an invitation says what they are and offers Import or Browse community. Uninstall on a skill's page has a background like its other buttons.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Space plays and pauses with the library in focus too (not in its search field): a thing just added with its + is no longer added again.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A person who speaks in a generated clip speaks with its own sound, the voice and the lips made together. `openfilm get video --voice <recording>` gives whose voice speaks the words written in the prompt (Seedance, at OpenRouter and fal), so a person keeps one voice across clips; `--audio <recording>` makes a person speak that recording word for word, the clip as long as it (fal: OmniHuman v2 or Kling Avatar v2 from a picture of the person, sync-3 for a clip's lips). Its help says to never lay a voice over a clip made without it; Gemini Omni and OpenRouter say where a clip speaking a recording is made.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - The library lists animated stickers before the emoji (their groups too), draws its cards a page at a time as it is scrolled, and shows a sticker whole in its card.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A sticker, text or effect dropped on the main track goes over it, on a track above, never cutting the film; what is dropped is picked, the try-out picture goes, and the timeline and Layers call a thing of the library by its name (an emoji by its own, not "emoji").

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Subtitles: a transcribed line dragged on the subtitle row, by an edge or whole, goes into the line next to it and cuts that one short (to a tenth of a second at least). It no longer stops at the neighbor's edge, which with lines that touch left no room to move at all. Lines heard from another clip are still never overlapped.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A template goes in as one clip on the timeline and plays as its example: its places hold example pictures made for that template, under its titles, transitions, effects and music. Picked, the inspector shows everything it offers: its places (click one to choose your video or picture, drop one on it, Choose clips for all of them, or Fill), every word, its style (one color for every part that shares it), layers to show or hide, pictures inside its pages, and its music. Each change is one step, undone as any other. A video or picture dropped on the template's clip on the timeline goes in the place playing there. Double-click still opens it to edit inside, and it can be taken apart. Every template, filter, effect and transition in the library has its own example now: each template its own story, each filter its own photo, each effect its own scene, each transition its own pair of pictures. Tried in the viewer or put in, filters, effects and transitions act on your film.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A template's places can be framed: pick one in the inspector to replace what is in it, fill the picture or fit it whole, zoom in, move the point looked at, and for a video choose where it starts. Its colors change all at once in a scheme (Original, Warm, Cool, Black and white, Neon, Soft), and a font it offers is picked from the fonts. Made longer or shorter, from Length in the inspector or by pulling its clip's edge on the timeline, its places stretch while its opening and its end keep their timing. Another template can take its place from the inspector, your clips and the words you wrote moving into it. Each is one step, undone as any other.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A template put in from the library stays one template: clicking any of its clips shows it first in the inspector, with how many of its places still wait for your own pictures, each place numbered in the order it plays with its length and what is in it now (click to choose one of the project's videos or pictures, or one from this computer; or drop one on it), Fill to put the project's pictures in every place still waiting in one step, every word it says in one list, and Select the whole template to move it as one. Its music goes under the film's tracks. A filter or effect card shows a picture at the playhead as itself.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A project's copy of the library's line of words is brought up to the library's own each time a line is put in, so the words' motions keep within their box in projects made before too. Every other thing taken from the library keeps the copy the project already has.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A line of words put in says the default words ("Default text", in the app's language), and the Text inspector shows them to be edited.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - The library's text category is laid out as New text, Mine (Favorites, by word art or templates, and My presets: the looks saved in a line of words' Word art, put on a new line with a drag or a double click), Word art and Text templates, each with its groups. Text motions are no longer listed there: they go on a line of words from the Text inspector. Word art shows on square cards, its words filling them.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - The Text inspector's font is a field like the others, easy to open; Left, Center and Right place a line in a box wider than it is.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A line of words grows taller or shorter while a side is pulled, not only once let go: its new lines are never cut, and the size by the pointer follows.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A new line of words shows at once where it is put in (no way in until one is picked). A motion picked, or its time changed, plays on the picture; the loop has a time too; a look or motion from the library replaces the last one and refits the box; a look previewed in the inspector refits the box as it goes; a split or trimmed line goes out at its end.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A line of words is one frame on the picture, its box always its words: while it is typed in, its frame follows the words (a line hugs them, one pulled to a width keeps it and grows taller), the whole clip stays in hand when typing ends, and the words, box and size are one undo. New words or a new look from the inspector refit it the same way, and its words' size reads in the film's pixels.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A font file of one's own (.ttf, .otf, .woff, .woff2) can be added from the Text inspector's font field: it is put in the project with its lines of words and listed under In this project, so it goes with the project. An exact frame waits for a font a clip's changes name, so an export no longer draws a line before its font has loaded. A new line of words says "Default text" whatever the app's language, as the library's things say their own words in English; a moving text template or sticker fills its card in the library.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Save preset in Word art keeps a line of words' look to reuse; your presets are shared with the captions' and come first.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A line of words' look previewed in the inspector (a font hovered, its size dragged) refits its box from what is written, never from the last preview, so the words no longer grow without end; its frame follows the preview. Double-clicking a line of words types in it with its whole clip in hand, so the inspector and frame no longer flash.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A line of words pulled narrower or wider by a side grows or shrinks to as many lines as it now takes, its words as large as they were and its middle where it was, in one undo. The Text inspector shows its values again whenever they change from elsewhere (a pull on the picture, an undo).

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Each text style's card in the library and in the inspector's Word art draws the style itself, its words filling the card in the app's language, in place of a small picture of English words; the cards are larger, and saving a look as a preset sits beside the presets.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - The library's text styles and word art drag onto the timeline as a new line of words in that style, as a click adds one.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A text style or motion tried from the library shows the words a new line says, in the app's language, at the size and place it gets when put in, and a sticker tried shows at its put-in size too. A line of words breaks only where a new line is typed: one wider than its box is made smaller, never wrapped. Text motion cards draw their words and play the motion under the pointer; moving stickers fill their cards.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Typing in a line of words keeps it steady: its box is fitted to the words in the picture itself as each is typed (never a frame of shrunk or wrapped words), a line aligned left or right grows from that side, the words keep their size when typing ends, the Text inspector stays while typing, and a box's padding is of the words' size.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Words typed into a line of words on the picture always stay: they are kept in the one place the Text inspector reads, never under another name that its default words would cover.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - The timeline's record button is the first of its right side, before the switches; the Fit to width button is gone (⇧Z still fits the film). A line sets the record button apart from the switches, and another the switches from the zoom.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A track's sound button in its head is a speaker, crossed out while the track is muted.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A sound goes only on a sound track (A) and a picture, page or line of words only on a picture track (V): dropped on a track of the other kind it goes on the nearest of its own free there, else a new one where its kind's tracks are, and a clip is not moved onto a track of the other kind. A line of words is drawn as its words wherever it is.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Studio: a track's head menu has Delete track, which takes the track out with all on it in one undo step. A caption track left with no caption goes, as a track left with no clip does. The canvas's code is fetched while the editor is idle, so the first switch to the canvas shows it at once instead of a blank.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Chinese and Japanese transcripts no longer keep the spaces a transcription service puts beside full-width marks or between characters (`OpenFilm， 说` is `OpenFilm，说`); Korean, and a space between Han and Latin, stay as they are.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A video's or sound's transcript (and its translations) beside it is no longer listed in the media drawer as a file of its own: the media is marked instead, and its inspector shows the transcript's lines with their times. Nothing on disk changes, and a transcript still moves and is renamed with its media. A WebVTT with no media of its name is still listed, and previews as its lines. The drawer's player stands on the chat's composer again, and a side bar heading has a light fill only while the line shown is under it.

- [#320](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/320) [`452d117`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/452d117c6a412c6e3e76f8c259cb0f9ab2680b8b) Thanks [@rick-mingyu-liu](https://github.com/rick-mingyu-liu)! - Studio on Windows: an item the Recycle Bin cannot take (on a network or removable drive, bigger than the bin of its drive holds, or a bin set to delete right away) is no longer deleted for good without a word. Deleting it is refused with the reason, and the file, folder, project or skill stays where it is, for File Explorer to delete.

- [#349](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/349) [`92a48d4`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/92a48d40d5a408631a6c89d25bf6031b692b9fda) Thanks [@WEBGRS](https://github.com/WEBGRS)! - Film: a trim to a file's end, written to the millisecond (`#t=0,2.167` for a file of 2.166667 s), is its end, not past it. Such a film failed to export, as a video and as a project for another editor.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A click inside a line of words being typed in places the caret there: its frame is only drawn while typing, not pressed.

- [#335](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/335) [`f594a19`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/f594a1964c073ef71fcecad57180e293b084fb74) Thanks [@WEBGRS](https://github.com/WEBGRS)! - Subtitles: "um" is printed where it is a word. A transcript that reads as Portuguese ("um dia") or German ("um acht Uhr") keeps it; elsewhere it is still left out as the English hesitation.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A video the preview can't play, though the browser said it could (HEVC on some Windows computers), gets its playable copy first, as one the browser says it can't decode does. And a render whose browser never shows video frames, with no other way left to show them, fails with the reason instead of drawing the film black.
