- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - `openfilm get video --video <clip>` makes a clip from another in the project, the model's way, chosen with `--model`: at fal, Kling O3 Edit changes what the prompt names and keeps the rest, Kling O3 Reference makes a new clip with its camera and motion, Seedance 2.0 takes it as a reference, Luma Ray 3.2 redraws it with its performance kept, and MiniMax H3 Max Recast puts the people of `--input-reference` in place of its own. The clip goes to fal's storage (a small one inline when that cannot be done), and the cost Studio asks about is for its length. `openfilm get video-search` finds video clips on the web at Pexels or Pixabay (1 to 4, `--orientation`), each downloaded near Full HD with its length, size and credit; Settings → Services lists it under video generation, with an icon of its own; the kinds that make media are named for it there (music generation, sound effect generation, image generation, video generation).

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - Adding a sticker, an effect, a filter or a template no longer flashes the picture: the film loaded again behind it comes up only once its first frame is drawn whole (its videos on screen, its pages drawn), the picture it replaces stays over it until it is on screen, and a film written again while it loaded is read again instead of failing into a black "can't be drawn" picture.

- [#370](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/370) [`4287d3c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4287d3c8fb480534634c3d2c556e4b6307175838) Thanks [@riverisbuilding](https://github.com/riverisbuilding)! - A WebVTT in the media drawer is listed as the file it is again, beside its media, and previews as its lines with their times. A menu narrowed after it was built (a caption's clip menu) no longer starts with or doubles its dividing lines. In the desktop app the drawer's player ends right on the chat's composer, with no strip between.

## 0.2.7

### Patch Changes

- [#293](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/293) [`005f61d`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/005f61d76bbeefa364f0c5ef23d9155f17620b29) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Render: a video with an alpha keeps it. A WebM (VP9) or Apple HEVC with an alpha whose key frames are far apart was given a copy without its alpha for a render, which then showed what lay under the alpha (often a solid color) in the export, and in Studio's picture once the copy was made. An HEVC with an alpha that the browser does not decode now gets a copy with its alpha.

- [#277](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/277) [`015a240`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/015a240c8fe7ee10fbf8cf2814f654314060afcd) Thanks [@WeiXunWuMing](https://github.com/WeiXunWuMing)! - Exports: footage the browser shows no picture of is copied in a form it plays, instead of being drawn black. ProRes with sound, which most cameras record, plays its sound and shows no picture, without an error: it gets a copy too, as HEVC on a computer without a decoder for it already did.

- [#278](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/278) [`1ed02d7`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/1ed02d7e84e8e85a9a02af864e59be1518e0671c) Thanks [@rick-mingyu-liu](https://github.com/rick-mingyu-liu)! - Settings › Agents says when Claude Code is older than the one OpenFilm was tested with (2.1.308), whose model menu may then miss the newest models, and gives the command that updates it the way it was installed: npm, pnpm or bun, its Homebrew cask, or its own installer (`claude update`). The Claude app's own copy is updated by the app.

- [#317](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/317) [`74c47ea`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/74c47ea5a6207065bbefe3e0c219ed8653afcd2b) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Studio: a click on a layer, a clip or something in the picture only selects it. The small bar of buttons that came up beside the selection is gone, and everything it offered is in the right-click menu, which is complete everywhere. With a chat beside Studio, Reference in chat is always the first item, then a line, then the rest. A row of the Layers panel gives the same menu as that layer in the picture, and selects it; a clip's row gives the clip's menu. In the picture, a clip's menu has Crop and Mask, and several layers' Align has Distribute. On the timeline, a clip's menu has Duplicate, Mute or Unmute, and Speed (checked while on), which makes dragging the clip's edges change its speed instead of trimming it. With a range marked, the ruler's menu has Play range and Clear range. A right-click menu closes on any click outside it (the Layers panel's stayed open).

- [#298](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/298) [`71d732a`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/71d732ad241dcaa62edaff9b51705891e4f8267d) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Studio: layers inside a page can be copied and pasted, as in a design tool. Select one or more on the picture or in the Layers panel, then press ⌘C / Ctrl+C or choose Copy in the layer's menu (a Layers row's right-click menu has them too). Paste with ⌘V / Ctrl+V or Paste in a menu.

  The copy is a new, independent element written into the page's HTML right after the original, or last in its parent when the page's CSS would restyle what is next to it. It gets new ids (`<id>-copy`). The page's CSS for the old ids is repeated for the new ones in the page's head, and the clip's changes to the original (styles, moves, words) are written into it. Scripts and `on…` handlers inside it are left out, so the page's code runs once. It is placed 24 px lower and to the right, each paste at the same spot on top of the one before, and selected so it can be moved at once. From then on it is an ordinary layer: it shows in the Layers panel, moves, exports and is deleted like any other. When other clips also show that page, this clip first gets its own copy of the page (`<name> 2.html`), so the other clips stay as they were.

  Pasted while another page is in hand or under the playhead (also from another project), the layer is written into that page as it looks now, with its look written in. Files it uses from another project are copied into `pasted/<project name>/`. With no page under the playhead, it goes back into its own page. One undo removes a paste, the page and film.html together, even after the agent edited the page meanwhile. A page that is not UTF-8 keeps its encoding. A paste pastes whatever was copied last: layers, or clips on the timeline. The Layers panel also reads the page again when a page file changes.

- [#317](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/317) [`74c47ea`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/74c47ea5a6207065bbefe3e0c219ed8653afcd2b) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Studio: a layer dragged in the picture is let go with nothing else flashing up. Once the drag was written, an outline came up where the drag had begun, often a frame around the whole picture, and stayed until the pointer moved; dragging a layer again and again made it blink on and off. Now only the layer in hand keeps its frame.

- [#318](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/318) [`c7be858`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/c7be85877df0ab4a2a1197981e3220c933f21733) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Studio: the clip under the pointer on the timeline has a small round handle just inside each end, on the bottom of its picture (where a video's sound starts, or the bottom of a page or a still) or on the top of a sound clip's waveform. Drag one inward to fade the clip in or out; where the handle sits is where the fade ends, and nothing else is drawn for it. A hairline across the clip's sound is its volume: drag it up to make the clip louder or down to make it quieter, with the level shown as you drag, one undo step. The line sits a little above the middle when the clip plays as is, and is barely there until you point at it. The clip's waveform is drawn as loud as it plays, and grows or shrinks with the line as you drag it, and when the volume changes from the inspector, Mute, Undo or an agent; its top is full scale, so the peaks a volume pushes past it are cut off at the top with their clipped part in orange, and a muted clip draws a flat line. This is true of sound clips and of a video's own sound. The handles and the line show only while the pointer is on the clip, selected or not, and stay while one is dragged; a selected clip the pointer is not on, or clips selected with a box, show none. A clip is trimmed by dragging its lit edge itself: no bar shows on its edges any more. While Speed is on, dragging an edge changes the speed, and the clip's frame turns the accent color to say so.

- [#295](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/295) [`10b8da1`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/10b8da1ac3b382a1679e762e97f35bc32ce5e5f0) Thanks [@rick-mingyu-liu](https://github.com/rick-mingyu-liu)! - Studio: a video or sound clip's speed goes from 0.1× to 100× (it was 0.25× to 4×), its sound's pitch kept in exports. The inspector sets it on a slider marked 0.1×, 1×, 2×, 5×, 10× and 100×, with the number beside it, the clip's new length shown as you drag, and a reset (or a double-click) back to 1×. Dragging a clip's edge with Speed on (in the clip's right-click menu) reaches the same range. In the preview, a clip the browser can't play that fast or that slow (past 16× or below 1/16×, its speed by the viewer's) shows its picture frame by frame, without its sound; the export has both. A film with a speed outside 0.25×–4× doesn't open in OpenFilm 0.2.6 or older.

- [#316](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/316) [`7b0acde`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/7b0acded22360662e6f778949b2e00d811704293) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Studio: a project whose film.html goes missing while its folder is still there stays open. An agent that deletes film.html and writes it again a while later no longer closes the project's tab and stops the agent with it. The picture says film.html is missing, and that it may be being rewritten; once it is back, everything goes on as before. A project whose whole folder is deleted or moved still closes. (#304)

- [#316](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/316) [`7b0acde`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/7b0acded22360662e6f778949b2e00d811704293) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Studio: Version history opens, lists its versions and restores them while film.html is missing, so a film an agent removed can be brought back exactly when it is needed. (#304)

- [#312](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/312) [`40a0f0d`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/40a0f0d7dfd6ea1cd0cc0cb61909a122c5caf218) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Keenable for web search and reading pages, under More in Settings › Providers, beside Brave Search and Tavily. It works without a key, so Connect connects it at once; a key added afterwards lifts its limits. Like every provider it serves nothing until it is connected, and the ? beside "Web search" in its row says that the searches and the addresses read go to Keenable.

- [#317](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/317) [`74c47ea`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/74c47ea5a6207065bbefe3e0c219ed8653afcd2b) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Studio: in the Layers panel, ⌘-click (Ctrl-click) adds a layer to the selection or takes it away, and Shift-click selects every row shown from the last one clicked to it (with ⌘ or Ctrl held too, it keeps what was selected). Rows selected one after another now join into one muted blue block, rounded only at its ends, and the layers inside a selected group are tinted with it; a row under the pointer is lined. A box drawn on the picture is filled more lightly.

- [#305](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/305) [`0cb9a65`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/0cb9a650d33d7019b5d2e59977b3daccbcc5c093) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Agents put a new clip on a track that has room for it, beside clips like it, instead of adding a track each time, so a film no longer ends up with many tracks.

- [#317](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/317) [`74c47ea`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/74c47ea5a6207065bbefe3e0c219ed8653afcd2b) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Studio: when a page changes under the selection (an undo or redo, the agent's write, the page loaded again), layers that are gone are taken out of what is selected and no box is drawn where they were, while the layers still there stay selected. Undoing a paste no longer leaves boxes where the copies were, including the outline under a pointer resting on one. When the page of a clip is opened again, as an undo of a paste that gave the clip a page of its own does, the selection waits a moment for it instead of letting go of everything.

- [#316](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/316) [`7b0acde`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/7b0acded22360662e6f778949b2e00d811704293) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Studio: no version is saved while film.html is missing, by hand or on its own. Such a version held none of the project's files. Saving says why: film.html is missing, so restore it first. (#304)

- [#284](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/284) [`97bc98b`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/97bc98b9d2d9585ea917b24abc9742c68b5f0a27) Thanks [@WEBGRS](https://github.com/WEBGRS)! - Settings › Agents: three of an own model's errors are worded as people say them in 日本語, 한국어, Español and 简体中文: nothing answering at the address, the status the service answered, and a model left out.

- [#311](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/311) [`0ea0d0e`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/0ea0d0e8ba6b36708834eaa43b1ccde1070eac45) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Timeline: ⌘V pastes onto the track under the mouse, at the playhead. Before, it went onto the selected clip's track, which is usually taken at the playhead, so the copy landed on a new track and had to be dragged into place. Clips copied from several tracks keep their rows from the track under the mouse down. With the mouse elsewhere, ⌘V works as before, and a locked track under the mouse now says so.

- [#317](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/317) [`74c47ea`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/74c47ea5a6207065bbefe3e0c219ed8653afcd2b) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Studio: pasted layers are all selected together. After copying several layers (a box drawn around them, or Shift-clicks) and pasting them, every copy is selected, not only the first, so one drag moves them all, as do the arrow keys and the inspector's fields. A single pasted layer is selected alone, as before.

- [#301](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/301) [`8cdc274`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/8cdc27478362fc262766cd7a15703f43a2846466) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Studio: what you change while an agent works stays. An agent often writes film.html whole, from a copy it read before your edits; Studio now merges that write into the film as git merges, from the version the agent's copy was: what only the agent changed is taken, what you changed since stays, and where you both changed the same thing, yours stands. You are never asked anything. The agent is told what stood over its write, in its next `openfilm` command (on stderr) or the chat's next message, so it can read the film again. Subtitle edits and the page links Studio rewrites are merged the same way, line by line. Every merge keeps the agent's own file aside in the project's history first.

- [#314](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/314) [`716aeb1`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/716aeb19a374c126eb7a30bd9947d6cb9314f8be) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Studio: a layer you paste into a page stays when an agent writes that page from a copy it read before, and so does an undo of a paste; the agent is told the page stood.

- [#292](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/292) [`6c7d599`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/6c7d599c2c7e98d8f05a9d56edfc775f0a83771f) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Studio: the viewer plays at 3× and 4× too, its sound’s pitch kept. Its playback menu is laid out again: loop and speed as the layout menu’s rows, and the speed two rows, 0.25× to 1× and 1.5× to 4×. J/K/L’s 4× plays with sound, and the full-screen player offers the same speeds.

- [#310](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/310) [`d6e5b78`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/d6e5b785f705b60849f65e3ef772eb14ec3283db) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Studio: a video your browser can’t decode, such as HEVC from a DJI drone or a phone on a PC without an HEVC decoder, no longer shows a black picture. The viewer says “Preparing this clip to play… 42%” over it while Studio makes a copy it can play, and the media pane shows the same. These copies are made before any other, even with proxies turned off in Settings, and the picture appears as soon as one is ready, without reloading. A copy that can’t be made says why. Exports still use the original.

- [#316](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/316) [`7b0acde`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/7b0acded22360662e6f778949b2e00d811704293) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Studio: while film.html is missing, restoring a version from Version history brings back film.html alone, as that version has it. Every other file stays as it is, so an agent's other work since that version is kept: nothing is written over or sent to the Trash. With film.html there, restoring works as before: the current edits are saved first, then the whole version comes back. (#304)

- [#317](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/317) [`74c47ea`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/74c47ea5a6207065bbefe3e0c219ed8653afcd2b) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Studio: Shift-click on the picture adds a layer to what is selected, or takes it away; ⌘-click / Ctrl-click on a Layers row does the same. Shift with a box drawn on the picture adds the layers in it. The Layers panel now shows every selected layer as selected, and a Shift-click on the one layer selected, inside its own box, takes it away instead of starting to move it. Two Shift-clicks in a row no longer start typing into a line of text.

- [#287](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/287) [`d71b72e`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/d71b72eda800c94ea6bb2de6747baea630cb0d50) Thanks [@HEOJUNFO](https://github.com/HEOJUNFO)! - Studio: a new project starts in the subtitle look used last, written to it as it is made, so burned-in subtitles match what Studio shows; an opened project shows its own look or the default, as its exports do

- [#318](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/318) [`c7be858`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/c7be85877df0ab4a2a1197981e3220c933f21733) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Studio: a page clip on the timeline keeps its pictures while they are made again after a change, whether you moved or restyled something in it or an agent wrote the page or film.html. The new pictures replace the old ones all at once when they are all ready, so the clip no longer flashes empty, showing only its color.

- [#317](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/317) [`74c47ea`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/74c47ea5a6207065bbefe3e0c219ed8653afcd2b) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Studio: clicking or drawing a selection box in the dark area around the picture no longer tints the whole picture blue. The browser was selecting the film's video and page as if they were text; the viewer now only selects what you click or box.

- [#300](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/300) [`a17102c`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/a17102cf042bcf1fad1da69c2c77a82d6d457ba1) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Studio: the picture in the viewer zooms and pans. Pinch on a trackpad, or hold ⌘ (Ctrl on Windows) and turn the mouse wheel, to zoom about the pointer; while it is zoomed, scroll (Shift + wheel: sideways) or drag with the middle button to pan. The zoom shows in the viewer's bar, and pressing it fits the picture again. Selecting, moving and resizing on the picture work at any zoom, with handles the same size on screen. Once a zoom has settled, the film is drawn again behind the picture for its new size, so canvas and 3D pages are as sharp as they are seen, past the film's own size up to 64 megapixels a page; a drag, words typed in place or a crop meanwhile carry on, and the new drawing is shown when they end, with what they kept. When the film is drawn again (after a zoom or a resized viewer), the picture no longer flashes blank. The zoom changes only the viewer, never the film or its exports.

- [#294](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/294) [`768fc6d`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/768fc6d4dfdb5bd9922a6f89bf43b659eb0942f5) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - What's new after an update shows only the version now running, not every earlier release under it; the changelog on GitHub keeps them all.

## 0.2.6

### Patch Changes

- [#271](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/271) [`e754142`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/e7541420eba3b62bfff9243ee4dc5a7f1fc45488) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Identify connection components separately from installed desktop apps, open Claude for Code setup, and use a single installation-folder picker to find an existing connection program. Put a help icon at the end of each agent's description, opening connection instructions, manual location and opt-in diagnostics in a dialog. Keep connection actions and visibility switches directly accessible. Discover agents in the background and publish each result immediately, prioritizing enabled agents and checking sign-in only for those in use. Load model options only for the selected agent. Refresh connections re-reads configuration, renews idle sessions and model menus, and clears errors from the previous configuration without interrupting running turns. Discovery and protocol checks do not send model test requests.

- [#245](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/245) [`4981238`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/49812381b40cf5cfa8ce57e5d506a98ae3027f23) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Restore the agent sign-in button when its login terminal closes, and let the user cancel waiting for sign-in from Settings.

- [#263](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/263) [`257dd8d`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/257dd8de7307d8b905f826d55a3ef4baa17b6849) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Switch account for the chat's agents. Claude Code keeps its own sign-in, apart from the Claude app's, so switching accounts in the Claude app did not change the one OpenFilm used. Settings → Agents now has Switch account: Claude Code, Cursor and MiniMax Code sign out with their own command and open their sign-in for another account, and their chats carry on with the new one; the other agents say where they switch (the Codex app, the DeepSeek Harness app, or the agent itself). A turn that fails for its account (signed out, an expired or refused sign-in, an organization that turned off Claude subscription access) offers Switch account and names the account signed in. Cursor's row shows its account's email.

- [#220](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/220) [`dfa9849`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/dfa9849e3d1b220d2b5820099e9eed0efd899f11) Thanks [@WEBGRS](https://github.com/WEBGRS)! - Studio: a film whose first picture is a page laid out at the canvas's size (most pages an agent writes) shows its canvas as what it is (16:9) instead of "Original". "Original" is the shape of the first picture with a size of its own: a video, a still, or a page that sets its size.

- [#221](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/221) [`4dfa8fe`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4dfa8fec3d33bfc99985ced33a250539fd8db066) Thanks [@WEBGRS](https://github.com/WEBGRS)! - Settings › Agents: a personal Claude account reads "email · Max", without its organization, which is only "email's Organization" and said the email twice.

- [#168](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/168) [`619c163`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/619c163d2632e6e87e989049fec04dfc22188cd1) Thanks [@ZhangYukwiver](https://github.com/ZhangYukwiver)! - Find custom Jianying and CapCut draft folders when their settings quote paths containing commas or semicolons, so exports go to the folder the app uses. Also read escaped paths from older Qt settings.

- [#270](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/270) [`f17330f`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/f17330f2f6a897f13b35ec17c16fa1d6a2eab014) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Studio: layers inside a page can be deleted, not only hidden. Use the bin on a row of the Layers panel, Delete in the layer's menu (one layer or several), or Delete / Backspace while a layer is selected and no text field has focus. The page file is not changed: the clip gets a `display: none` override for the layer. Its other changes, the changes of the layers inside it and the component properties made from them are removed in the same step. One undo brings everything back. The deleted layer no longer appears in the Layers panel.

- [#186](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/186) [`04f6d10`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/04f6d10b07ef6324d8bcc58eef9c3954b5c67771) Thanks [@WEBGRS](https://github.com/WEBGRS)! - Desktop: the sign-in window hints for MiniMax Code (international accounts pick their region with `mcode login --region global`) and CodeBuddy (its window is titled CodeBuddy) say what to do.

- [#254](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/254) [`e923057`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/e923057dc414a8495b6ff31d78d5557f2afaf629) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Drafts for 剪映 and CapCut: the film's subtitles now arrive as the app's own subtitles, on a subtitle track in their language, like the subtitles it recognizes itself. You can find, restyle and edit them together, instead of as separate text clips.

- [#205](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/205) [`10eacd7`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/10eacd784ddec04e05877d35fb35490f5366cfdb) Thanks [@WEBGRS](https://github.com/WEBGRS)! - Media services: an account whose plan doesn't include what was asked (ElevenLabs' free plan, for music or a voice from its library) is told so, with the service's own reason, instead of "doesn't have enough balance", which sent people with credits left to top up, or "did not accept your key", which sent them to check a key that works.

- [#264](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/264) [`985dca6`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/985dca657cb67ed93706b97734ff67de913144f6) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Export: pick the exact frames and ranges to export, in a larger dialog that grows with the window.

  - Images and Slides & PDF are one page, Images, with four ways to deliver the same frames and framing: image files, a ZIP, a PDF or a PowerPoint deck. Any frame can be picked, not only the middle of each shot. The film shows large, as it will be exported, over a filmstrip that zooms and scrolls like the timeline. Click or drag to look, then Add this frame or Return; ← → step a frame. Quick picks add the playhead, the middle of each shot, each marker, or a frame every few seconds. Each image is named after the shot it is in, and the picks are remembered for each project.
  - A partial video, GIF, audio or subtitle export can take up to 100 ranges. Add a range, trim it on the filmstrip or type its start and end (seconds, M:SS, H:MM:SS or a frame timecode), and play it in the preview. Export each range as its own file, or merge them into one file in the order listed, with picture, sound and subtitles kept together. A range that is not valid stops the export with a message; the whole film is never exported in its place.
  - Video file has the social media presets: Default first, then each platform. Changing a setting makes it Custom. Saved social export settings carry over.

- [#257](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/257) [`02dc438`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/02dc438b1b18ba58524da16de9733f250cb1863e) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Exports: a page's pictures and fonts are in every frame. A page opened during an export was drawn once its `window.film` was ready, and a page whose `ready` did not wait for its images lost them for some frames. An exact frame now waits for the page to finish loading (at most 30 s, then it is drawn as it is and the page is named among the export's problems).

- [#175](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/175) [`cf6cd6f`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/cf6cd6fa9b3d4ee50dd963853ee6ca4f8354ecf4) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Exports: a video whose browser crashed or stopped answering is drawn once more in a new one, as an editing project's pages already were. A page or a file the browser could not load for want of the system's network buffers (`net::ERR_NO_BUFFER_SPACE`, seen on Windows) is drawn again too, instead of failing the export.

- [#252](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/252) [`0ca8c95`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/0ca8c951c34409cf8422fbad0f4d0e1ac91f0a2f) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Exports keep their sound more reliably. "Include sound" is on each time the export opens: switched off once, it used to stay off, and every later video came out silent without anyone noticing. A film counts as silent only once every video in it has been checked, so the Audio export no longer says a film has no sound while its videos are still being read. A file made again at the same path is checked again for sound, instead of being remembered as silent until Studio restarts.

- [#213](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/213) [`3037c7d`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/3037c7d16078b724ec9046b857008b8a8a65976a) Thanks [@WEBGRS](https://github.com/WEBGRS)! - Projects: a folder's card is drawn as a folder, with its tab at the top left, so a folder holding one project no longer looks like that project's card. Its size and the grid stay as they were; the list view's folder thumbnails have the tab too.

- [#216](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/216) [`ec7590f`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/ec7590f03c1ab79801982d54fc195a63d6508c40) Thanks [@WEBGRS](https://github.com/WEBGRS)! - CLI: `openfilm get` ends with its own exit status on Windows with Node 24.17. It used to print what it got and then abort (`Assertion failed: !(handle->flags & UV_HANDLE_CLOSING)`, exit status 127), so an agent read a good answer as a failure.

- [#194](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/194) [`14a47bf`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/14a47bf1daab861f0deb0d1f084a36415da3960a) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - `openfilm get call --path` also takes the path without its leading slash (`--path speech` is `/speech`), so it works from Git Bash on Windows, which turns an argument starting with `/` into a folder path. A path Git Bash turned that way now gets its own message: drop the leading slash or put `MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1` in front of the command.

- [#267](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/267) [`179fb68`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/179fb68f62a3b1384d7c2d52168cd3bdd70e048b) Thanks [@yinbowen-dc](https://github.com/yinbowen-dc)! - Studio: opening a film from GitHub keeps files and folders whose names start with two dots, such as `..assets`. These names are no longer mistaken for paths outside the project.

- [#265](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/265) [`e052d6e`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/e052d6e3a3bf3dd53e9f0df03fdbfc18cda4fc02) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - A render no longer waits forever on a graphics chip that hangs while it copies footage the browser can't decode or seek well (VideoToolbox does on some virtual Macs). A copy on the chip that writes nothing for 15 seconds is stopped, with no ffmpeg left running, and made on the processor; the chip's encoder is left out for the rest of the launch.

- [#266](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/266) [`585860e`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/585860e20e78f0cd5ba750a0fd1c120ae23a9188) Thanks [@yinbowen-dc](https://github.com/yinbowen-dc)! - Studio: on Linux, moving files with the same name to the Trash at the same time keeps every file and its original path. A failed move no longer leaves a record for a file that was not trashed.

- [#210](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/210) [`cb731fd`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/cb731fd9acd7c6ecf371b05e69b339bea6648347) Thanks [@philipp-reisich](https://github.com/philipp-reisich)! - `look` and `render` keep going when more than six videos of long files are awake at once: an open-ended read of a video is at most 2 MiB, so a paused video reads its piece to the end and holds no connection, and a frame-duration lookup given up on after ten seconds is no longer reported as a failed request.

- [#233](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/233) [`cba20bb`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/cba20bbc1fe7b097c3cbd923bc51918e08301411) Thanks [@rick-mingyu-liu](https://github.com/rick-mingyu-liu)! - `look` of a 4K film no longer fails with "Target page, context or browser has been closed". A film's contact sheet now embeds each frame at the size of its cell, as a media file's sheet does, so twelve detailed 4K frames no longer pass the 100 MB a page can be given. The full frames are still compared as before.

- [#269](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/269) [`8472808`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/8472808770820ad7f6f667a77bc72498e8940935) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Studio: a mask can have a stroke. In the inspector's Mask section, add one and pick its color, its width and where it lies against the mask's edge: inside, center or outside. It follows every shape, inverted ones too, scales with the clip or layer, and shows the same in the preview and the export. It is plain CSS: an SVG filter that draws the stroke, plus one mask layer for the part past the edge. Safari does not load this kind of filter, so a preview in Safari shows no stroke. For a center or outside stroke, the extra mask layer still applies there: the band past the mask's edge where the stroke would be shows the picture instead. The desktop app, Chrome and exports draw the stroke.

- [#261](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/261) [`1b2ce64`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/1b2ce647745583ed89801d50e927fc2c428a330e) Thanks [@laoer536](https://github.com/laoer536)! - Xiaomi MiMo writes down what is said, for subtitles, and speaks a line, with your own key: connect one in Studio (the OpenFilm logo at the top left → Providers) or set `MIMO_API_KEY`, then choose Xiaomi MiMo for transcripts or voice-over, or run `openfilm get asr --via mimo --src assets/talk.mp4` and `openfilm get tts --via mimo --out intro --text "…" --voice Chloe`. Its speech recognition times no words: the transcript's lines spread over the recording, and Studio cuts them into subtitles as it does any other.

- [#256](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/256) [`c0faee2`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/c0faee2ca2349bd6a0487324f59d8814286c0a12) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Exports: in an editing project (XML, FCPXML, 剪映 or CapCut draft), two files whose names differ only in how an accent is written (`café` in its two Unicode forms) each get a copy of their own, as two that differ only in case already did, so neither clip loses its footage on macOS.

- [#193](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/193) [`168fe66`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/168fe66a486aa50fddfb642734ea283f1c1554f4) Thanks [@WeiXunWuMing](https://github.com/WeiXunWuMing)! - Preserve source timecode and its DF/NDF display format in Premiere Pro and DaVinci Resolve XML exports, including full video conversions. Sequence starts and source-relative trims stay unchanged; rendered clips, audio segments and stems keep their zero start.

- [#240](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/240) [`dba3a04`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/dba3a04f4688a83186d05c8f99e304ffe08a97a4) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Studio: a long project opens at once. The playhead moves and plays from the start, and the picture comes in about a second. A page whose length its clip gives opens when the playhead comes near it, rather than every page before the first frame (9.5 s on a 6-minute film of 371 clips, now 0.2 s). The colored line under the ruler is gone: what plays late is still rendered in the background, without showing it.

- [#250](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/250) [`53a6d7f`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/53a6d7f37f60ae9a2cbee6371e56197018202529) Thanks [@WEBGRS](https://github.com/WEBGRS)! - Settings › Agents: when an own model is not saved, the reason is said in the app's language (简体中文, 日本語, 한국어, Español), not only in English: an address that is not https:// (or http:// on this computer), a model or key left out, an address that is a web page or that nothing answers at, a key refused, a model that cannot read pictures, a service out of reach. What the service itself says is shown as it said it.

- [#212](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/212) [`037b128`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/037b128e149e42368b191bbb1c47a56d8ce696fa) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - On Windows, a page the system briefly had no network buffer for (`net::ERR_NO_BUFFER_SPACE`) is loaded again, in `look`, Studio's thumbnails and exports, instead of failing. A project's card no longer misses its film's poster when it is asked for at the moment the poster is being written.

- [#255](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/255) [`45ed894`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/45ed894154dfb75e4b6d56d197565e975c64a34d) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Exports: a copy of the project (zip or folder) takes the files the film uses from a linked folder, as files at the same places, with their subtitles and transcripts, so the copy plays. The rest of a linked folder still stays out.

- [#245](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/245) [`4981238`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/49812381b40cf5cfa8ce57e5d506a98ae3027f23) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Refresh project availability when the library opens and when returning to the app. Check again before opening a project, and leave open editors when their folder or film.html is removed externally, with a short grace period for file saves. Missing project files are distinguished from empty films, and removing unavailable projects from the list preserves any remaining files.

- [#251](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/251) [`69f70b8`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/69f70b8ff47723a88dda1083411a88606c77caf2) Thanks [@WEBGRS](https://github.com/WEBGRS)! - Exports: a ProRes video's likely size runs from what a title card comes to (about a twenty-fifth of ProRes's rate) to what detailed footage comes to (its rate). A simple film was shown at about ten times its size.

- [#219](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/219) [`3ec2351`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/3ec2351abed8ee39553db324140a231607eb63cf) Thanks [@WEBGRS](https://github.com/WEBGRS)! - On Windows, deleting a project, a file or a skill says it goes to the Recycle Bin (回收站, ごみ箱, Papelera de reciclaje), where it does go, instead of the Mac's Trash.

- [#245](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/245) [`4981238`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/49812381b40cf5cfa8ce57e5d506a98ae3027f23) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Remove the timeline's preview axis switch, hover preview and keyboard shortcut. Click or drag the timeline playhead to seek and preview the film.

- [#196](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/196) [`a5a6a04`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/a5a6a041fb38165069d4b65e823774b50cb7554d) Thanks [@mvanhorn](https://github.com/mvanhorn)! - Rendering a long edit keeps going. An open-ended read of a video is at most 8 MiB, so it cannot hold every connection to the film, and a frame is still drawn when asking how long the video's frames last does not answer within ten seconds.

- [#183](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/183) [`28206c3`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/28206c376e0d398e69df7b541b3edb1313fd47b7) Thanks [@WEBGRS](https://github.com/WEBGRS)! - CLI: `openfilm render --subtitles` burns the project's subtitles into the rendered video, in their own style, through the same code Studio's export uses.

- [#195](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/195) [`42c46e6`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/42c46e6ed5cdfafeb1abd4a91f6b61f7c6ddff25) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Desktop: a click on the timeline's ruler moves the playhead and a drag scrubs, as in other editors. To refer a moment to the chat, ⌘-click the ruler (Ctrl-click on Windows), the key that draws a box on the picture; ⌘-drag refers a span. While the key is held the cursor turns into a crosshair and the moment under it is drawn.

- [#200](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/200) [`ce2e120`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/ce2e120066874e631c8f8c7d8ddc99d1deea2bcd) Thanks [@WEBGRS](https://github.com/WEBGRS)! - In the app, with no project open, the logo at the top left opens Settings. The projects window's shade covered it, so a click on it did nothing. The top bar now stays above the shade, in the window's color.

- [#184](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/184) [`66e6715`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/66e671589c297debb5a27a7e8cf971615299306f) Thanks [@WEBGRS](https://github.com/WEBGRS)! - Subtitles: each line can fade in and out (Fade in and out under the subtitle panel's Effects, `fadeMs` in the style; off by default). The editor's preview and the burned-in export draw the fade the same way.

- [#245](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/245) [`4981238`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/49812381b40cf5cfa8ce57e5d506a98ae3027f23) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Keep the ruler's normal pointer until the reference modifier is held. Show referenced ranges only on the ruler, and briefly highlight ranges recalled from chat while seeking to their start without changing the editing selection. Empty space in the subtitle row seeks and scrubs, and the timeline's floating reference button is removed.

- [#258](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/258) [`aa29fc8`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/aa29fc82945e4f26de33d004dfc56b3d005f965a) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Exports no longer come out black where a video can't be played. A video larger than 4096 on a side that the export's browser does not decode (ProRes from a camera larger than 4K, say) gets a copy scaled down to fit, as smaller ones get a copy at their size. A copy that could not be made is said in the render's log, and a video the browser still can't decode now fails the export, naming its clip and file, instead of drawing black frames and saying it is done. Studio's preview is unchanged.

- [#264](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/264) [`985dca6`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/985dca657cb67ed93706b97734ff67de913144f6) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Version history is redesigned, in a window the size of Export.

  - Versions are listed by day, with search and a filter by type (manual, auto-saved, restore, other) or named versions only. Current edits are at the top, with the files changed since the last saved version, and Save version (an optional name) when something changed.
  - Selecting a version plays its film from the saved copy and lists the files it changed. Looking at a version never touches your current edits.
  - Restore this version brings the project back to it. Unsaved edits are saved as a version first, so nothing is lost. Markers, settings and subtitles follow the restored version in every window. Save as new project starts a separate project from any version.
  - Delete moves one or more versions to Deleted, where they can be recovered. The version in use can't be deleted, and no file or saved copy is removed.
  - Discard all changes returns the film to the last saved version; files added since go to the Trash.
  - A version keeps all of the project's files (pages, code, data, fonts, notes), not only the ones the film names. Unused video, sound and pictures, and files over 50 MB, are still left out. An older history is brought up to date the first time it opens.
  - Settings › Advanced › Versions › Auto versions (off by default) saves a version before and after an agent's turn and when the film rests for two minutes, only when the film changed.
  - Branches and merging are gone from the window; versions saved on earlier branches stay in the list.
  - When the history can't be read, it says so, with Try again.

- [#247](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/247) [`6fb7e83`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/6fb7e83e20d1c28f7192c575c3a077debe945b3e) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Exports on Windows: when Vulkan doesn't show video, OpenFilm now asks for Direct3D by name. Before, it fell back to Chromium's default, which on Windows draws on the CPU. And when a film's video frames never show in the browser drawing it, though the check made at startup passed, the export is drawn again in browsers that show video another way, instead of running for hours to make a black video. The export tray keeps the time left in view when its line is too long.

- [#264](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/264) [`985dca6`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/985dca657cb67ed93706b97734ff67de913144f6) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - What's New is taller, and below this update's notes it scrolls on through every earlier release, in the language Studio shows, so the whole history reads without the English changelog on GitHub (still a click away). A release can now list up to 24 changes there instead of six.

- [#271](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/271) [`e754142`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/e7541420eba3b62bfff9243ee4dc5a7f1fc45488) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Find the agent programs bundled with OpenCode and GitHub Copilot on Windows, and DeepSeek Harness installed in a custom folder. Recognize registered desktop apps outside their default folders, including MiniMax's icon-only registration, so an installed editor is distinguished from a missing command-line agent. Choosing the DeepSeek Harness, OpenCode, or GitHub Copilot desktop executable now locates its bundled or app-managed agent. Find MiniMax's Windows CLI at its install root and keep its own launcher so native SQLite uses the correct Node runtime. An agent that cannot be found is no longer described as not installed.

- [#211](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/211) [`27432f0`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/27432f0095eb88d3facdc8aed5d475b8ea77ec47) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Exports on Windows: on some computers with an NVIDIA GPU, Vulkan never showed the video frame a seek asked for. Video clips came out black, or an export drew one frame every five seconds, as with HEVC footage such as DJI cameras record. OpenFilm now checks once that an H.264 and an HEVC frame show as sought. If one doesn't, it uses the same GPU through Direct3D, or keeps Vulkan and decodes video on the CPU.

## 0.2.5

### Patch Changes

- [#163](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/163) [`0cc33fe`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/0cc33fe34f0ef98ec5a511d700ff4c8b73e1ce02) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Export › Burn in subtitles: bilingual subtitles show the original and its translation once each, as in the editor. The translation was drawn twice.

- [#160](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/160) [`ef8c6c3`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/ef8c6c31be3c65421e53bc3d9bcc27d6655079f6) Thanks [@taavie-z](https://github.com/taavie-z)! - Keep selection action bars behind Export and other dialogs, so clip actions cannot be pressed through the export window.

- [#151](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/151) [`4df3346`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4df3346442c7c4c80ebcf4e53a9a21ba79bbfb48) Thanks [@WEBGRS](https://github.com/WEBGRS)! - Projects: double-clicking a folder opens the folder only. Before, its second click opened whichever project had just appeared under the pointer.

- [#163](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/163) [`0cc33fe`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/0cc33fe34f0ef98ec5a511d700ff4c8b73e1ce02) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Editing-project drafts are faster and need almost no spare disk space. Each page's transparency is read in every frame as the page is drawn. An opaque page is written straight to H.264 (or ProRes 422 HQ), with no lossless copy first and no second pass. A page with any transparent pixel is drawn again at once in the same browser tab, as lossless PNG (or ProRes 4444), exactly as before. A draft of a music video whose pages were held as gigabytes of PNG while it was made now needs no more room than the draft itself.

- [#163](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/163) [`0cc33fe`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/0cc33fe34f0ef98ec5a511d700ff4c8b73e1ce02) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Export › Editing project › 剪映 or CapCut: a draft goes to the drafts folder the app's own Settings › Drafts location names, so it is on the app's home screen even when that folder was moved (it went to the default folder, where the app no longer looks).

- [#163](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/163) [`0cc33fe`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/0cc33fe34f0ef98ec5a511d700ff4c8b73e1ce02) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Export › Editing project starts its browsers once for all the pages and keeps them steady.

  - One process draws every page with the same browsers. Each page opens its own tab and closes it after.
  - A browser that crashes or stops answering is replaced at once, and its page is drawn again in new browsers. A page's own error still stops the export and says why.
  - A browser is started again after many pages, so memory stays flat.
  - Cancel still stops everything and leaves nothing behind.
  - A transparent page now comes out the same to the last pixel however many browsers share it. Before, the first two frames each browser drew came out a level or two off.

- [#163](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/163) [`0cc33fe`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/0cc33fe34f0ef98ec5a511d700ff4c8b73e1ce02) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Export › Editing project is two to four times faster, with the same files. A 剪映 or CapCut draft's pages are drawn as a quick lossless PNG and written once at the end (H.264, ProRes, or PNG as before): ffmpeg's own PNG settings took more processor time than drawing the page. Pages of every editing project are drawn several at once with the browsers the export already uses, each page's check and re-encode running while the next ones draw. On an M5 Mac a 50-second film of ten full-frame pages went from 153–190 s to about 50 s, and a 95-second music video from 334 s to 145 s.

- [#163](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/163) [`0cc33fe`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/0cc33fe34f0ef98ec5a511d700ff4c8b73e1ce02) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Export › Editing project: a choice of how the pages are written. A 剪映 or CapCut draft is automatic by default: each page is drawn with its transparency, as Studio shows it, and every frame is read; a page with no clear pixel in any frame comes as H.264 (the graphics chip's when there is one), and one with any transparency as lossless PNG, each picture kept once for as long as it shows. A one-minute film of full-frame pages went from 2.5 GB to under 20 MB. All PNG, or ProRes (422 HQ for an opaque page, 4444 for one with transparency), can be picked instead. Premiere Pro and DaVinci Resolve keep ProRes 4444 by default, with PNG to pick; Final Cut Pro takes ProRes 4444.

- [#163](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/163) [`0cc33fe`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/0cc33fe34f0ef98ec5a511d700ff4c8b73e1ce02) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Exports keep the page's colors in every codec: ProRes, VP9, HEVC and the editing-project pages are converted with BT.709 and tagged so (red came out up to 25 levels off, green up to 39, in Apple's apps), with the graphics chip or without it. HEVC with alpha opens in Apple's apps again: it was copied after encoding, which dropped its alpha layer.

- [#163](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/163) [`0cc33fe`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/0cc33fe34f0ef98ec5a511d700ff4c8b73e1ce02) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Exports need far less free disk space while they run. A page's frames were on the disk three times while its file was made; now they are there twice at most, and a page drawn by one browser is moved into place instead of copied. This matters most for editing projects, whose pages are drawn as lossless PNG first, so a long page is gigabytes. An export that fills the disk now says so plainly, in your language, instead of showing ffmpeg's log.

- [#163](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/163) [`0cc33fe`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/0cc33fe34f0ef98ec5a511d700ff4c8b73e1ce02) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - An editing project's browser pool and its footage copies stop their whole process tree on Windows when they are ended, and a copy that a virus scanner holds for a moment as it is written is tried again instead of counting as not made.

- [#163](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/163) [`0cc33fe`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/0cc33fe34f0ef98ec5a511d700ff4c8b73e1ce02) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Quitting Studio while an export runs no longer leaves half-made files behind. Studio now waits a few seconds for the export to stop and clean up after itself. If Studio was ended outright (forced to quit, a crash, the computer shutting down), the next export cleans up what was left: hidden half-made files beside your exports and drafts, and scratch in the project's cache, which could be gigabytes.

- [#163](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/163) [`0cc33fe`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/0cc33fe34f0ef98ec5a511d700ff4c8b73e1ce02) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - The export tray stays until it is closed: a finished export's card no longer goes by itself after a few seconds, and it is still there when the project or the page is opened again while Studio runs, so its file is always one click away. A closed card stays closed.

- [#163](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/163) [`0cc33fe`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/0cc33fe34f0ef98ec5a511d700ff4c8b73e1ce02) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Exports are safer on Windows and when something goes wrong: a file is written under a hidden name until it is whole and then renamed, so a stop or a crash never leaves a broken file that looks made; a rename that a virus scanner or the editor watching the folder holds for a moment is tried again; cleaning up after an export that is made never fails it (it used to delete the finished files); a cancel ends the export's whole process tree; a long page's PNG keeps its pictures however many there are; the graphics chip running out of memory or sessions falls back to the processor's encoder; names ending in a dot or a space, or named like a Windows device, are written so Windows can open them; and a draft's files are named with the system's own separators.

- [#163](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/163) [`0cc33fe`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/0cc33fe34f0ef98ec5a511d700ff4c8b73e1ce02) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Footage the browser cannot play (ProRes, and other codecs it has no decoder for) placed whole, without a trim, plays and exports: its length and picture size come from ffprobe when the browser cannot read them. The film stopped with "no length to trim against" in Studio's preview, in every export and in `openfilm render`.

- [#163](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/163) [`0cc33fe`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/0cc33fe34f0ef98ec5a511d700ff4c8b73e1ce02) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Export: a mono sound, such as a voice recording, is as loud in the export as in the editor. It was 3 dB quieter.

- [#150](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/150) [`419da5d`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/419da5dc483267b07e4f32d73c993fe5f8ee04d1) Thanks [@WEBGRS](https://github.com/WEBGRS)! - Projects: a folder has its own "New project", and the project it makes is in that folder. Before, a project could only be made at the top level and then moved into a folder.

- [#163](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/163) [`0cc33fe`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/0cc33fe34f0ef98ec5a511d700ff4c8b73e1ce02) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - The paused preview shows the exact frame of a video, as an export draws it. The browser sometimes decodes a frame and never puts it on screen, so the picture could stay a few frames behind after you stepped or stopped. Studio now checks the frame on screen and seeks again if it is not there yet. Scrubbing stays as fast as before.

- [#163](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/163) [`0cc33fe`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/0cc33fe34f0ef98ec5a511d700ff4c8b73e1ce02) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Viewer › Playback settings no longer has a subtitles switch. Subtitles are shown or hidden only with the CC button, in full screen too.

- [#159](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/159) [`d864efc`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/d864efcb23352d80f8c74a0645602555933e70bd) Thanks [@taavie-z](https://github.com/taavie-z)! - Keep subtitles and their translations attached when renaming or moving audio and video files in Studio.

- [#163](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/163) [`0cc33fe`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/0cc33fe34f0ef98ec5a511d700ff4c8b73e1ce02) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Exports drawing several pages at once make the playable copy of shared footage once. ProRes, HEVC a browser can't play, and slow-seeking footage get that copy. Two pages of one source now wait for the same copy, instead of both writing one file, which could leave a page drawn from a half-written copy or, on Windows, from footage the browser can't play.

- [#163](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/163) [`0cc33fe`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/0cc33fe34f0ef98ec5a511d700ff4c8b73e1ce02) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - The inspector's text color now changes words whose page gives them a fill of their own (`-webkit-text-fill-color`, often used for gold or gradient words). It shows the color the words are drawn in, and a color you pick takes over that fill, so the words are drawn in it.

- [#163](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/163) [`0cc33fe`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/0cc33fe34f0ef98ec5a511d700ff4c8b73e1ce02) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Inspector › Volume is in dB, from silent to +14 dB (500%): 0 dB is the file as it is, and each step sounds as big as the next, quiet or loud.

- [#163](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/163) [`0cc33fe`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/0cc33fe34f0ef98ec5a511d700ff4c8b73e1ce02) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Inspector › Volume goes up to 500%, and Normalize loudness can raise a clip that far, so a quiet voice recording can be as loud as the rest of the film. It stopped at 200%.

## 0.2.4

### Patch Changes

- [#148](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/148) [`0a90640`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/0a90640fa5412ab5501daeeb8f01e83691b9de76) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - The app's own agent (your own key) keeps a long conversation going when it looks at many frames. Pictures keep their full quality. A request carries the pictures of the agent's latest five reads, and at most 32 MB of them; an older one is left out of the request with a line naming its file, so the agent can read it again (the conversation keeps them). A model service that refuses a request as too large (HTTP 413, a proxy's page included) is tried again at once with half of the older pictures left out, and says so in the chat; before, every message after it failed the same way. Settings › Agents says that the agent's rules are the project's own AGENTS.md.

- [#146](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/146) [`29346ca`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/29346ca43b426a014faebafcebe3e8ce3a738faa) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Sound played faster or slower is heard the moment the speed changes, and sounds natural. The browser plays it at that speed with its pitch kept, in the same mix, kept in step with the picture; nothing is made on the server first. 0.2.3 re-encoded the whole sound for each speed, which left long videos silent for seconds after a change and made voices sound hollow.

  The viewer's playback button shows a speed gauge instead of the inspector's icon.

## 0.2.3

### Patch Changes

- [#139](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/139) [`b12dbdc`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/b12dbdce341b62a442e2b38e43b121adf7c26d4a) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Settings › Providers › Microsoft Azure sets up each Azure service on its own, since Azure has no one key for all of them: Azure AI Speech (voice-over) takes its resource's region and its key in two fields, instead of one `<region>:<key>` to type.

- [#114](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/114) [`f90e8ab`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/f90e8abb389c096a6a318341c01ea5643596ebf8) Thanks [@taavie-z](https://github.com/taavie-z)! - Choose cover images when adding skills, and replace or remove covers for your own skills from their details.

- [#120](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/120) [`e199314`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/e1993148e81402f78dcd72687c188b8fee582d18) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Settings › Agents: Claude Code waiting in the Claude app shows only how to get it (open the app's Code tab once), without an Install button that led elsewhere.

- [#145](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/145) [`f6b1df1`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/f6b1df146ee5582ad3dc884f6c2eb821b7a05f40) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Page clips have component properties. Select a layer and click ◇ beside its words, its picture or its appearance to make it a property; the clip's Properties section lists only those, each renamed with a double-click, filled in for this clip, and reset on its own or all at once. Variables of the page can be added from the section's +. A clip added to Saved keeps its properties and shows as a component, and every clip taken from it offers them. The Template section that listed everything a page holds is gone.

- [#140](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/140) [`b6b632a`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/b6b632a6a74730deeaf28d42b8a3d6c0a030c7ea) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Export › Editing project › 剪映 or CapCut: when the app's drafts folder refuses the write (on Windows, security software often does), the draft is made in the exports folder instead of the export failing with "EPERM: operation not permitted, mkdir". The export says so, and where to move it.

- [#141](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/141) [`c344c1f`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/c344c1f53f32331784aa8ebbaa437216ee0c233f) Thanks [@WeiXunWuMing](https://github.com/WeiXunWuMing)! - Restore the media pane's right-click menu when the library or a folder is empty, including over the empty-state message. Existing file and folder menus keep their own actions.

- [#145](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/145) [`f6b1df1`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/f6b1df146ee5582ad3dc884f6c2eb821b7a05f40) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Studio: Export in the top bar is a button that says Export. A chosen project's outline shows whole, never cut by the edge of the list. Settings › Keyboard shortcuts lists the keys without a panel behind them.

- [#127](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/127) [`4503390`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/4503390dcb31c421948a2a517cb040c9e6cf043d) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Studio: scrolling in the export dialog no longer makes the window flicker on macOS. Nothing behind the dialog is blurred, and a scroll that reaches the end of a list stops there instead of moving the page behind.

- [#145](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/145) [`f6b1df1`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/f6b1df146ee5582ad3dc884f6c2eb821b7a05f40) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Moving a project now opens a folder tree that shows where everything is. Folders can be moved too, with everything they hold.

- [#132](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/132) [`b8bda5e`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/b8bda5ea66dd5801a19c5704ceb4661fa89fe2a9) Thanks [@kefamgaya](https://github.com/kefamgaya)! - Transcribe audio for captions with Gemini 3.5 Transcribe and a Google Gemini API key, including word-level timing.

- [#138](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/138) [`32efde3`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/32efde3ef065aaefd71fd6ae3f87bb09e9f93b27) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Google is drawn with Google's mark in Settings, instead of Gemini's.

- [#123](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/123) [`9728bae`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/9728bae004c0c879c307a44c2dcfa388d5187acf) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Studio: keyboard shortcut schemes. Settings › Keyboard shortcuts lists every editor action in tabs (Timeline, Player, Basic, Other) and lets you switch between OpenFilm's keys, Premiere Pro's and Final Cut Pro X's. Three custom schemes take any keys you record: changing a key in a built-in scheme copies it to a custom one first, and a key already in use asks before it moves. Menus and tooltips show the keys of the scheme in use.

- [#145](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/145) [`f6b1df1`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/f6b1df146ee5582ad3dc884f6c2eb821b7a05f40) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Projects: "Move to folder" says where the project goes. Its title is "Move “name” to"; the first place is the project list itself, not in a folder; each folder says how many projects it holds, and the one the project is in now says "Here now". A new folder is named in the field at the bottom, and Create and move (or Enter) puts the project in it.

- [#129](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/129) [`008edef`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/008edef0ebd9c54fcc8b09896886a516a0e7b077) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Projects: one click on "New project" makes the project and opens it, named Untitled (Untitled 2…); rename it later. Going back to Projects no longer spins forever: a card whose picture is still being drawn shows its color and asks again, instead of holding Studio's connections while the next page waits, and a list that can't be read says so, with Try again.

- [#85](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/85) [`d6a3a86`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/d6a3a86ba9903a60e3f83f9dd74bd9dc90a54cfb) Thanks [@xyd945](https://github.com/xyd945)! - Keep live film, file, history and export updates connected after a project is renamed, and send the desktop agent to its new folder. Wait for active agent work and running or queued exports to finish before renaming a project.

- [#145](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/145) [`f6b1df1`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/f6b1df146ee5582ad3dc884f6c2eb821b7a05f40) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Studio: deleting projects, the open one among them, no longer says "Delete failed" and puts them back when they were deleted: the list is read again, and only a project still there comes back. The top bar's Export is an icon again.

- [#136](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/136) [`711e994`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/711e994b91f04540868b362a48cd3b3b84c644af) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Providers are named for their makers: Google Gemini is now Google, and Azure Speech is Microsoft Azure, in Settings, in the key dialog, and in what `openfilm get` says.

- [#133](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/133) [`9bbdf42`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/9bbdf42f8d86adaa1e93431a30b24624f7f0c470) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Settings › Providers shows the recommended services first (OpenAI, Azure, Google Gemini and ElevenLabs), and the others under More providers, folded until opened. More opens by itself when one of its services is connected.

- [#113](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/113) [`1da84e3`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/1da84e34e6fa50653449630379958d3585f6ab45) Thanks [@taavie-z](https://github.com/taavie-z)! - Refresh the open skills gallery after adding a skill, without reloading the page.

- [#145](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/145) [`f6b1df1`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/f6b1df146ee5582ad3dc884f6c2eb821b7a05f40) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - A picture inside a page can be replaced right on the picture: Replace picture on its bar and its menu, or drop a picture from the media pane onto it. A frame holding one photo replaces the photo. A layer's inspector has a Picture section with its thumbnail.

- [#145](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/145) [`f6b1df1`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/f6b1df146ee5582ad3dc884f6c2eb821b7a05f40) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - A page clip with a size of its own (a portrait scene in a landscape film) is drawn laid out at that size in its thumbnails and in its picture in Saved, as the film shows it. A page saved to Saved now plays in the viewer, at its clip's size and with the changes made in it, instead of showing a still.

- [#122](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/122) [`ef79883`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/ef79883ff7032258534ea24726a06f12ff28d15b) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Settings: "Draw pages at the viewer's size" and "Render what plays late" move from General to a new Advanced section under Help. Both stay on, as they were.

- [#137](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/137) [`aebde17`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/aebde17786af60c536a2b3a6fc3be225ec6acde5) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Settings: Playback (proxies for heavy footage and their size) moves to Advanced too, so General keeps only what most people change. The proxies' line says 4K is among the footage they cover. The projects folder shows its path without the line about it.

- [#145](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/145) [`f6b1df1`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/f6b1df146ee5582ad3dc884f6c2eb821b7a05f40) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Skills are installed, then kept on or off. Installed lists yours first, then the OpenFilm skills you installed, this project's, and your agent's own (off until you turn one on). Each has a switch: on, an agent takes it up whenever it fits; off, no agent gets it. Discover is OpenFilm's catalog: install one to use it. A skill opens as a page with its preview, what it does, a switch, Uninstall (or Move to trash for your own), and Try now, which starts a new conversation with the skill named. `openfilm skills` lists the same skills agents get. A skill named in a message is for that message only; its instructions go to the agent once per conversation, unseen in the chat.

- [#145](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/145) [`f6b1df1`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/f6b1df146ee5582ad3dc884f6c2eb821b7a05f40) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Sound played faster or slower in Studio keeps its pitch, at a clip's speed and at the viewer's playback speed, as it does in an export. Before, the preview sped sound up like a tape, so it was heard higher or lower.

- [#124](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/124) [`a7f0a1e`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/a7f0a1ef461f346ae73e53ca9eb7932f80bb2c39) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - On Windows, Open in Settings → General → Storage shows the projects folder again, and the button no longer looks greyed out. In the app, the top bar's "…" menu is now one Export button with an export icon; "Use this frame as the cover" and "Reveal in Finder" (Explorer on Windows) are in the project tab's right-click menu. A tab's close button shows a background when pointed at, without a tooltip.
  The timeline's preview axis starts off: the picture stays at the playhead until you turn it on.

- [#145](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/145) [`f6b1df1`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/f6b1df146ee5582ad3dc884f6c2eb821b7a05f40) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Projects and tabs keep in step. A project deleted (in the Projects window, another tab, by an agent) or whose folder is gone closes its tabs without asking, all of them together, and the front goes to a tab that stays; none of them comes back with Reopen Closed Tab. The Projects window never stays on "Creating project" or "Opening project", reads the list again whenever it is shown, and has the tabs in its top bar while any are open. Closing the last tab starts the next launch on the Projects window. Changes to the projects list made at the same moment (several deleted together, one made meanwhile) all hold, and a rename shows in its tab's title.

- [#135](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/135) [`8209ca4`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/8209ca45a5a1d1d067a926ba26f3e7b8b9222cb9) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Pressing Undo again and again no longer makes the editor disappear. An edit that cannot be undone, because the agent changed or removed what it was about since, says why once and leaves the history, so the next Undo goes on to the edit before it and Undo stops at the oldest one.

- [#145](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/145) [`f6b1df1`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/f6b1df146ee5582ad3dc884f6c2eb821b7a05f40) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - The viewer's loop button is now a playback settings menu: loop, subtitles on the picture, and playback speed from 0.25× to 2×. The aspect ratio button under the picture is gone; the inspector's Stage menu has every shape, with Original at the top.

## 0.2.2

### Patch Changes

- [#110](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/110) [`7d35283`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/7d352837b96754793d7c2e7c1d129656a29cfdba) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Desktop: agents work for people who installed only the maker's app: Claude Code and OpenCode from inside their apps, and an Install button that runs the official installer for the others. The Cursor editor is never started in place of Cursor's agent.

- [#98](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/98) [`f771d05`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/f771d053d31ef28fd0c21ce1ca5e848a632b2954) Thanks [@cristinamadureira353-svg](https://github.com/cristinamadureira353-svg)! - Studio: an export can be named where it is asked for — a name field beside the folder it goes to, the project's name in it to begin with — instead of every file taking the project's name. A named export keeps the suffixes that tell the files of one export apart (` · 哔哩哔哩`, `(alpha)`, `stills`) and drops the ones that only describe the project (`1080p`, `0-9s`). A name left empty is the project's, as before, and an export that would take a name already there still gets ` 2` rather than being written over.

- [#106](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/106) [`3582b88`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/3582b88e75099d0f9af9f927d5ae042826f5e88b) Thanks [@taavie-z](https://github.com/taavie-z)! - Keep Final Cut Pro subtitles white with a black outline and a soft shadow. Correct Basic Title font scaling so subtitle size stays proportional in low-resolution, 4K and portrait sequences.

- [#108](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/108) [`7192264`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/71922644da5d6b67db2cc796d4e057f98999944f) Thanks [@taavie-z](https://github.com/taavie-z)! - Resize media contact-sheet thumbnails before embedding them, preventing "Invalid string length" on high-resolution, high-bit-depth clips. Single-frame output keeps its original resolution. A transparent WebM (VP9 or VP8) keeps its alpha in the sheet and in a single frame.

- [#101](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/101) [`bc1ae78`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/bc1ae787dda20a9a7fe3e5b6d3f9aba8863cc2fc) Thanks [@PazChen](https://github.com/PazChen)! - Render: when an encode fails on Windows, the error says why instead of a permission error from cleaning up.

- [#107](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/pull/107) [`94ea4c8`](https://github.com/openfilm/openfilm/commit/94ea4c8237f933866e9ef5a7b41bdca4e64db4e2) Thanks [@taavie-z](https://github.com/taavie-z)! - Allow decimal frame rates such as 23.976, 29.97 and 59.94 in `openfilm render --fps`, keeping the existing range of 1 to 240. The NTSC rates render at their exact rates (24000/1001, 30000/1001, 60000/1001), as footage shot at them is.
